# The visible game programme: workflow spec (rev 12f, 2026-10-12: A17 budgets, the three-group starting force, S-235 ticker; rev 12e, 2026-10-12: HARNESS-userdir, wave-12 number corrections; rev 12d, 2026-10-12: E1k numbers, unitscale, prodslot, B24, B25, A18; rev 12, 2026-10-11: lanes W and F part 1, E1 part e, B23, the provenance lint; rev 11, 2026-10-11: lane E1 part d, lots on full frames (K-e6); rev 10, 2026-10-10: lane E1 part c, the settlement port after the S-237 rejection; rev 9, method rev 2 2026-10-10: skeleton first, pre-gate, ruler in the script, tests on the built tree, no token cap; rev 8 after the part a stops 2026-10-10 (K-e2 entry points), after the lane E pre-flight 2026-10-09; D8 spawn placement (Westley 2026-10-09))

Programme plan: `2026-10-07-visible-game-programme.md`. Rev 1 from the ten pre-flight scouts (`preflight_2026-10-07/`); rev 2 folded audit round 1 (Opus 15 issues; parallelism cuts); rev 3 folds audit round 2 (Opus, 16 issues, "approve with the listed changes"; the three cited tests and the reach-lint exemption re-checked on main). Audit reports: `audits_2026-10-07/`. Rev 3 went to round 3 (Opus, 10 must-change items, all text; verdict "approve with the listed changes"); rev 4 applies them and is the launch text, pending Westley's go line. Rev 5 adds the attach point rule to section 1 (from the 2026-10-09 drift check). Rev 6: lane E pre-flight 2026-10-09 (R-168-05 for the era building set; K-e bumps ABI and save versions with the three pin tests; place is a player command; E1j is the pins and ledger row; S-235 held; pipe split noted; 22 pieces).

## 0b. Rev 4 changes (round 3)

| Finding | Change |
|---|---|
| K0 proposed new getters that already exist (`om_unit_get_target_layer` :1917, `om_fog_get_drawn_sensor_circle_radius` :1530, `om_structure_get_hud_data` :1953, `om_unit_is_braced` :1908, `om_unit_transition_progress` :1916, `om_unit_ability_cooldown` :2029, `om_unit_in_backswing` :2047, `om_tick_read_ability_events` :2027) and declared no reader for fire/kill/capture events; `stage` undefined | K0 reuses those names; new symbols only: `om_tactical_event_count`, `om_tactical_event_get_at` (fire and kill produced in `unit.c`, capture in `tactical_support.c`; ability events read through the existing reader by the pipe only, once per call), `om_structure_count`, `om_structure_get_at` (`stage` 0 until E1), `om_objective_get_done`, `om_map_obstacle_count`, `om_map_get_obstacle_at`, `om_terrain_get_heights`; `acd` = now + `om_unit_ability_cooldown`, `acd_max` from the profile |
| Row 22's re-pin breaks its own control (`:97` rejects `state=1`; detector `!= 0` at `:57`) | Row 22 spells out the edit: `state` present and `state & ~7 == 0`; the control plants `state=8` and a missing `state`; this control change is permitted by the row |
| K-a's identity test cannot pass once `state` is non-zero and new keys move units into deltas | The test compares reconstructed full views with `state` zeroed and the new keys stripped, on a fixture with no deaths (the dead-slot scan untouched) |
| K-a and K-f both edit `om_pipe.c`/`c_bridge.gd` in parallel; K-h and K-e unordered | K-f starts after K-a's pipe and bridge pieces pass their check; K-h lands before K-e starts |
| G1's `look` needs a threshold routed to Westley | `look` ships warn-only (frames attached, metric reported) until the number lands; then it rejects |
| `fog_filter` set in the shared bridge would turn it on for tests (`_ensure_mission` :498-501; row 33 and `test_flow_spine.py:444-447`) | The flag is a bridge parameter, default off, set only by `tactical_view.gd`'s live call; rulebook row 38 for the flow spine (stands with the flag off) |
| The park rule had no mechanism | Every Westley-threshold check lives in its own `*_park_*` test file; the clerk leaves such files off the candidate, keeps them on `push/vg-parked`, lists them; the footprint fence only warns on owned files missing from a diff |
| The landing order in §1 read as a literal chain | Written as dependency edges (below) |
| "Lane dropped" undefined; a parked VG-VIEW link would park the chain | A lane is dropped when its clerk has no landable candidate after two check failures of non-parked pieces; contract double-rejects park dependents, they do not drop lanes; a parked VG-VIEW link hands `tactical_view.gd` to the next link |
| Stale programme text; `push/tv-read` unaddressed; E1h/E1i naming; save 8 twice; C10 file list; `state` bits as values; VG-CONTRACT must own whole files | Fixed below and in the programme; `push/tv-read` is source only (its test is adopted by A1/A5's writers or dropped); the wiring piece is E1i everywhere; save 8 lives in K-e only; C10 names its files; VG-CONTRACT owns the seven `.c` files whole for K-a's duration |
| Budget recount: about 117 per-file pieces (A 20, K0 9, C10 5–6, E1 22) | Section 8: expected about 115M, ceiling 140M; lanes C and E1 in two workflows each |

**K-a rulings, 2026-10-07 night (Architect, after the wave 2 reports):** (1) capture events are written from `om_sup_capture_accrue` after the owner change through a new internal symbol `om_tac_event_push(w, kind, a, b)` in `csrc/om_internal.h` (internal, not the frozen contract); `unit.c`'s ring is the single writer. (2) `acd` is present only while a cooldown runs; nothing is synthesised (rulebook row 43). (3) `tier` is the unit row's tier, stored at spawn from the faction tech tier until the roster carries per-unit tiers (lane E item; rulebook row 39). (4) `events_dropped` is emitted by the pipe when the ring hit its cap (the sim keeps the count per world without a getter). (5) The `fog.c` and `tactical_move.c` K-a pieces were no-ops (the getters already existed); `set_layer` reaches `om_command_set_layer` through the pipe verb added by the pipe piece. (6) Rulebook rows 39 to 44 rule the eleven transport reds the pipe piece listed. Wave 2c (`vg-wave2c-pipe.js`) builds the support ring writer, the pipe (rebased on K-f), the bridge, and then the pipe/bridge split.

## 1. Lanes, pieces, stories

A piece is one file, about 100 lines: tests (Engineer, a different agent) → build (Doer) → check (Engineer; C pieces and contracts) or the lane clerk's gate (Godot and docs pieces). Every lane ends with its story plays run and queued.

**Sequencing (dependency edges; a script may start anything whose edges are satisfied).** T0 (stage 3 + phase A landed; stale `pending` rows flipped): test writers for A, C, F, GUARD; GUARD builds G1–G4, G6, G7 (G1 warn-only); B0; the F build scripts. Edges: GUARD lands → every other landing; B0 result + F scripts land together; K0 → every lane's builds (against fixtures) and K-a; K-a's pipe and bridge pieces checked → K-f; K-a lands → A's workflows, E1i; A lands → A12; A12 → F5 (after K-f); C2–C4 land → C5 (after F5's slot in the chain) → C6, C10; K-e lands (E1's first piece; after K-h) → E1's tests and pieces → E1p (after C5); D's answers → D1–D4 → D5 (after E1i) → the D hook (after E1p); A4 lands → K-h. Pairs of disjoint green lanes land in one gate. Lab run 1 at night.

### Lane B: galaxy (`VG-B`, 11 pieces; look approved 2026-10-07)

Seen-reachable: S-106, S-107. Blocked: S-108 (router; after F). B0 pre-check (the 2026-10-01 stack vs main, 1,360 behind: merge-tree, rebase or rebuild, the A56-suspended checks listed; FIELD-EQ measured on the 80-region fixture) lands first with the F scripts; then B1 field + bake shader (FIELD-EQ parked if over 2%), B2 shell shader, B3a renderer, B3b labels + stand-in (iGPU cost measured), B4 wiring as a child node, B5 REFILL (parks at 12 ms), B6 flashes from events, B7 tween, B8 pull-back/push-in after F's router, B9 time-jump renderer (handoff from G-timejump).

**B22, the prototype's geometry (rev 10d, Architect 2026-10-10, after Westley rejected S-106, S-107, S-108: "why are we rebuilding this? ... doesn't look close to the prototype I approved"; rewritten after three fresh audits).** The galaxy audit of 2026-10-10 and its two follow-ups: the shell shader on main is byte-identical to the approved prototype (`origin/push/galaxy-field`, `scenes/mockups/galaxy/`, 99f35887) and main's sizes, camera distances and bands are the prototype's *unscaled* values (the bible's R-106-03 bands 75, 65, 40, 25 are those same unscaled numbers); what main lacks is the prototype's geometry: it packs the sim's 80 regions into a flat disc of radius 40 at minimum spacing 3 (`scenes/galaxy/galaxy_gen.gd:8-11`, `r = 40 * pow(randf, 1.6)`, Y jitter 3), where the unscaled prototype is a uniform ball of radius 100 at minimum spacing 22 for 180 to 220 systems (`galaxy_gen.gd:8-11, 44-61` on the branch). On screen main's stars sit about six times closer than the prototype's, so labels pile, filaments read as a thick tangle and shells merge. The 2026-10-08 shots also ran on the Compatibility renderer, which never builds the shell field. One piece, owner row `VG-B22` (depends_on VG-B, C2, C8, all attached; owns `scenes/galaxy/galaxy_gen.gd`, `galaxy_view.gd`, `orbit_camera.gd`, `territory_field.gd`, `shell_labels.gd` (one stale comment), `tests/test_vg_b_b22.gd`, and for the three ledger rows below `tests/test_vg_b_b1.gd`, `tests/test_vg_b_b18.gd`, `tests/test_galaxy_view.gd`):
- Layout (`galaxy_gen.gd`, `layout(region_ids, seed)` keeps its signature, sorted ids, one RNG): a uniform ball, `r = R_BALL * pow(randf, 1/3)` as the prototype (`:44-61` on the branch), `R_BALL` 71 (the prototype's 100 scaled by the cube root of 80 / 220 so the star density per volume is the prototype's; liberty, said in code), `MIN_SPACING` 22 (prototype `:11`) in 3D, `PLACEMENT_ATTEMPTS` 250 per id kept (main must place every id); a fallback placement after 250 misses is allowed, counted by `galaxy_gen.last_fallbacks()` and reported in `qa_state` as `layout_fallbacks` (the audit's model needed one fallback in 2 of 300 seeds); the fixture seed used by the tests must give 0, else the test writer picks the next seed and says so. `LINK_RADIUS` is not ported (edges come from the sim).
- Field: `INFLUENCE_RADIUS` 28 (prototype `galaxy_gen.gd:17` unscaled), `GRID_SIZE` 198 wu with `GRID_MIN` (-99, -99, -99) (`2 * (R_BALL + INFLUENCE_RADIUS)`, the prototype's rule `territory_field.gd:19`); `GRID_CELLS` stays 96 (the texture size, `territory_field.gd:20`), so the cell grows from 1.33 to 2.06 wu and the bake cost is unchanged; `INFLUENCE_RADIUS / cell` is 13.6 against today's 12 and the prototype tip's 10.5, inside the accuracy the grid-versus-exact check at `test_vg_b_b1.gd:510` demands (it follows `live.INFLUENCE_RADIUS` and the grid).
- Dots: radius `POINT_RADIUS` 1.4 (prototype unscaled 1.4; main's 1.2 was a draft) with the prototype's per-instance scale `(0.6 + 1.4 * sig) * lerp(1, 0.6, overview_weight)` (`galaxy_view.gd:228, 243` on the branch; the focus boost and flash factors stay out, they are B-lane pieces of their own), `overview_weight = 1 - blend_factor` when the band is overview, else 0 (`legacy_zoom_disclosure_controller.gd:24-46`), recomputed every frame in `_process` after `update_from_distance` (`galaxy_view.gd:181`; `blend_factor` moves continuously between 65 and 75 and the band signal fires only at the edges), as a per-instance transform update on the MultiMesh (today the dots are built once with an identity basis, `galaxy_view.gd:517-526`). Sizes of filaments, labels and the flash are unchanged (they are the unscaled prototype's already: 0.075 and 0.035, lift 6, pixel size 0.05, flash 3).
- Bands unchanged (splits 32.5 and 70, controller 25/40/65/75, bible R-106-03). Camera copied from the prototype unscaled (`orbit_camera.gd` on the branch, values divided by `CIV_SCALE`): default distance 50 (main's 72 sits in the overview blend; R-106-03 names Regional, 40 to 65, as the default), `DIST_MIN` 15, `DIST_MAX` 120, focus distance 28, default target the origin (main's (7, 0, 0) was a framing nudge for the disc). `qa_state().systems_px` lists only systems in front of the camera (`Camera3D.is_position_behind` false, the pick rule of `orbit_camera.gd:135`); with the camera inside the ball a system behind it has no pixel, and Godot's mirrored unproject of one is not a dot (B22 gate 2026-10-11, S-107 red at `S-107.py:31`). The TD-VG-B-12 fifth counts against the region count, not `systems_px.size()`. Ratio note: the prototype's default is 25 in a ball of 50 (`orbit_camera.gd:14`, `galaxy_gen.gd:8` on the branch), the port's 50 in 71; density parity was chosen over ratio parity, Westley judges it on the S-106 shot. The prototype's regional default is a close view of part of the galaxy, not the whole galaxy in frame; what Westley approved is that. Three tests encode the whole-galaxy framing the Architect's 10-08 look pass assumed, and move under one ledger row `TD-VG-B-12` (test-defect, assumption-changed, Architect 2026-10-10; `TD-VG-B-11` is taken): `tests/test_vg_b_b18.gd:96` (overview span 60 to 90 percent of the viewport width, counting off-screen systems; the upper bound is dropped and the lower bound 60 percent keeps the "small cluster on grey" failure caught), `tests/test_galaxy_view.gd:181-195` (`outside == 0`, every system inside the viewport; becomes "at least a fifth of the systems inside the viewport at the default camera": corrected 2026-10-11, the built geometry shows 23 of 80 at distance 50 and fewer when closer, the approved prototype about 21 percent at its default; the Architect's number, not a Westley threshold), `tests/test_vg_b_b1.gd:401-404` (`GRID_SIZE` 128, `GRID_MIN` (-64, -64, -64), `40 + r <= 64` pins; new values 198, (-99, -99, -99), `R_BALL + INFLUENCE_RADIUS <= 99`; the reference check at `:510` re-run). Old and new values named in the row.
- Tests first: `tests/test_vg_b_b22.gd` (positive control: the view instantiates with the 80-region fixture and `layout` returns 80 positions; then red until built: minimum pairwise 3D distance of the fixture seed's positions at least 22; Y spread exceeds 40; `INFLUENCE_RADIUS` equals 28; a tier-1.0 dot's instance scale exceeds a tier-0.3 dot's; the overview scale factor equals 0.6 at distance 80, 0.8 at distance 70 and 1.0 at distance 60, read from the MultiMesh instance transforms of a tier-1.0 dot after `qa_camera` sets the distance and one frame runs). `tests/qa/desktop/test_galaxy_pixels.py` re-runs (spacing 22 at about 18 px per world unit puts stars about 400 px apart; about 34 systems and 20 owned ones are on screen in the audit's model, so its 5 isolated samples and 5 colours hold; if the shell tint on a real GPU pushes dot pixels past its 40-per-channel tolerance, that goes to Westley, never to a ruling).

**A16, the layer order control (rev 12, Architect 2026-10-11).** `[W 2026-10-11: "An explicit layer select control was built in a prototype I approved"]` `[P scenes/mockups/layers/layers_mockup.gd:78-111 and its hud.gd:13-19, 63-64 (layers mockup, ladder 2026-09-22): keys 1 and 2 set the focus layer, Tab cycles it, Q and E transfer the selected units to layer 1 or 2, HUD buttons "To layer N"; left click selects, right click moves]` `[B R-001-07: a walker takes no layer order]` `[B R-002-01: a hard transfer begins on the same tick]` `[B R-103-04: the layer strip changes focus, not the camera]` `[B R-105-06 and Q-003-01: Alt+N narrows the selection to layer N, Alt+0 clears]` The tactical view gains the mockup's order control for layer-capable units, routed through the plumbing that exists and is unreachable today: `send_command({"kind":"set_layer","unit_ids":_selected,"layer":n})` to `c_bridge.gd:461, 476-477`, pipe verb `set_layer` (`om_pipe.c:1568-1571`), `om_command_set_layer` (`tactical_move.c:3126-3150`, which already ignores walkers per R-001-07 and returns -1 out of range). Control: the layer strip (`layer_view.gd`, R-104-09's right sidebar) gains one "To layer" button per layer under its focus markers (all five layers always, plain `Button`s with the layer strip's constants and the default theme, text "To layer N", stacked under the focus markers at the strip's width), and keys Q (one layer up) and E (one layer down) send the order for the selected units relative to the lowest selected unit's layer, as the mockup's Q/E did for its two layers generalised to five (`[DRAFT-a16-qe-relative]`; the mockup had two layers so Q/E were absolute, here they step; register row `Q-A16-01`: (a) step, default; (b) Q/E absolute to layers 1 and 2 as the mockup; (c) buttons only); Tab keeps cycling focus (`:995-996`); Alt+N narrowing (R-105-06) is unbuilt on main and is not A16 scope (`FINDING-alt-narrow-missing`). The view holds engine numbers (0..4) everywhere and adds 1 only in player text: the button labelled "To layer 3" sends engine layer 2. A selection of walkers only (walker source `EraConfig.movement_kinds()`, `autoload/era_config.gd:42`): the buttons are dimmed and the key does nothing (R-001-07; `last_command.ok` false, reason "walkers"). Q at engine layer 4 or E at engine layer 0 sends nothing and sets `last_command.ok` false, reason "range"; with any modifier held Q and E do nothing; a mixed selection of walkers and flyers sends every selected id with `last_command.ok` true; an empty selection does nothing. `scenes/settings/settings.gd` lines 195-199 (the keybinds card) are rewritten to "Ability P", "Layer Up Q", "Layer Down E" in the same change. `qa_state` gains `focus_layer` (from `LayerView.focus_layer()`, an engine number) and `last_command` gains `layer` (an engine number) when the kind is `set_layer`; the `qa_state` unit entries (`tactical_view.gd` :470-480) gain `tl` and `ld` from the frame's unit object. Play: a new lane A play `plays/A/test_S_002_hard_transfer.py` on the space era (flyers): every mission unit spawns on engine layer 0, so the play selects, presses Q (one layer up) and asserts within 25 ticks a unit with `state & 4` and `tl` equal to the layer above (`[B R-002-01]`), screenshot queued (S-002 "Changing layers takes time" is the story). Owns `scenes/tactical_c/tactical_view.gd` (A16 lands first, no VG-E1 builder is editing the file; VG-E1's remaining pieces rebase on it, Architect 2026-10-12), `scenes/tactical_c/layer_view.gd`, `tests/test_vg_a_a16.gd`, `plays/A/test_S_002_hard_transfer.py`, `docs/northstar/story_reviews/A.md`, `scenes/settings/settings.gd` (lines 195-199 only). **A17, the scroll plays measure game time (rev 12, Architect 2026-10-11; Westley: "more of a model doing the testing issue than an issue with the game's behaviour").** `[B R-103-09: pan speed stays the same on screen, so ground covered per second grows as the camera zooms out]` `[W 2026-10-11: TD-VG-A-1's doubled contact budget rejected]` Measured 2026-10-11 (scout, xvfb, seed 1, space era): the game obeys R-103-09 (`tactical_view.gd:955-979`, 600 px/s divided by the world scale; zoomed fully out the pan is 11x the opening speed), but under the plays' harness (`--fixed-fps 30` at 2 to 4 software fps) one wall second is 0.075 to 0.13 game seconds, so every wall-clock window in `tests/qa/desktop/plays/A/play_support.py` (`_edge_scroll_to` :117-153 polls 0.2 s wall and stalls on 3 s wall; `step_until` :156-181) sees about an eighth of the real pan rate, and the plays never zoom out before a cross-map scroll as a player would. Piece (test writer, an engineer; no product file): `_edge_scroll_to` measures progress and the stall in game ticks read from `qa_state().tick` (stall = no camera movement over `SCROLL_STALL_TICKS` 60; cap = `SCROLL_CAP_TICKS` 400, `[DRAFT-scroll-cap-ticks]`: 22,900 wu at the fully-out speed 13,333 wu/s is 1.7 s, 400 ticks is 20 s game time, a 10x margin that still fails a camera stuck at the opening zoom), and before a scroll longer than one screen it zooms fully out with wheel notches (as the player does, and only when `ground3d.present` is false), scrolls until the camera is within the half-screen extent at the restored scale of the target on each axis, then restores the previous zoom by the same count; a target not on screen after the restore is a play failure with both positions; the helper logs wall and game seconds per scroll in the report. The restored contact budgets (3000 and 4000 ticks, the pre-TD-VG-A-1 values) stay; if first contact at seed 1 still arrives after the budget (measured 5382 ticks with the order to the nearest enemy; `DRAFT-ground-contact-budget`: space-era measurement reused for ground; Westley's number), the play reports it as a finding `FINDING-contact-distance` (armies start 22,900 wu apart and meet 4.5 minutes after an attack-move) and stays red; A17 lands with S-134 and S-136 red under `FINDING-contact-distance`, listed in `lane_plays_known_red.txt` under that row by the rule in `rules.md` section (b); budgets and spawn distance wait for Westley; that distance is a mission-generation question for Westley with context (register row `Q-A17-01`: (a) spawn the forces closer, a number he gives; (b) the budget follows the measured distance; (c) as today), not a play change. The pan speed number itself: 600 px/s and the 4 px edge are placeholders (`camera.gd:4`, Q-103-04 open); the scout's table (opening zoom about 15: 890 wu/s, 26 s to cross the map in real time; fully out 1.7 s; fully in 69 s) goes to Westley with Q-103-04's candidates; no product change in this piece. The dead `scenes/tactical_c/camera.gd` (unreferenced by the view, used only by `tests/test_vg_a_camera.gd`) is finding `FINDING-dead-camera-gd` for a later cleanup with parity. Owns `tests/qa/desktop/plays/A/play_support.py`, `test_S_134_shots_and_hits.py`, `test_S_136_deaths.py`. Row `VG-A17`.

**C-s004 play fix (rev 12, Architect 2026-10-11).** `[B R-001-05: a walker ordered to a place on another layer takes the slope when it is on the shortest route]` `[B R-004-06: on a slope the unit's transition progress is its position along the slope, not a timer]` `[B R-190-03: a slope carries a bottom edge on the lower layer, a top edge on the upper layer and a midline halfway along its length]` The lane C play `plays/C/s004.py` is red with "spec gap, no layer-change order" (`lane_plays_known_red.txt:3`), but no order is missing: a walker crosses layers only by a slope on its route (R-001-07), and the play's right-click target `(1700, 900)` is a screen point on the same layer. The test writer (an engineer, not a builder) changes the play to regenerate the terrain from the mission seed through the pipe dump (`csrc/pipe/pipe_dumps.c`) as its oracle (no product change); the target is the nearest slope (by the units' centroid) whose bottom edge lies on the units' band, and the play clicks on that slope's top edge (R-190-03 gives both edges), so the route must cross it; if no such slope exists for the seed the writer stops with the gap. The detector is a selected unit's layer changing to the slope's upper layer with its position past the midline (state bit 4 is the flyer-transfer signal R-002-01 and says nothing about walkers). The window is the route length over the unit's pace plus 200 ticks, measured and recorded as `[DRAFT-s004-window]` (the writer adds that ledger row). If no selected unit reaches the slope's upper layer on a route that must climb, that is a sim finding (R-001-05 unmet), reported with the premise dump (unit layer, target band, route length), not a play change; the known-red line is removed either way and the play's result is the lane's record. Owns `tests/qa/desktop/plays/C/s004.py` and the known-red file line. Row `VG-C-s004`.

### Lane E1 part a2: the K-e2 amendment (rev 8, 2026-10-10; Architect ruling after the part a stops)

Why. The part a run (wf_4b9a1642) stopped six of seven builders on one gap: K-e froze the economy state and its getters but gave the economy no entry from the tick, the mission setup or the structure code, and named `economy_init`/`economy_step` for E1i without declaring them anywhere. E1e also found `om_economy_place` stubbed in `economy_lots.c` while the spec gave it `economy_place.c`. E1g found no mix numbers (Q-179-02 open), no setter, and a conflict between the K-e default (balanced) and Q-233-01 (unset until chosen). K-e2 closes the first two gaps as an internal contract (no public header change, no ABI or save bump); E1g is narrowed.

**K-e2 (`VG-CONTRACT-E2`): `csrc/economy_internal.h` plus `csrc/economy.c`.** Internal, never in `ouroboros_sim.h` (precedent: `csrc/space_internal.h`, K-d2). Every function below is declared in the header; the contract piece defines each with an empty (no-op) body in the file named, so the tree builds and the wiring is live before the builders fill them. Builders fill bodies in their own file only and never change a signature.

Entry points (`csrc/economy.c`, fan-out only, no logic):
- `void om_economy_init(om_world *w)`: called once from `om_mission_setup` (`mission.c`), right after `om_structure_assign_owners`, every era. Order: `om_density_init(w)`, `om_lots_init(w)`, `om_waves_init(w)`, `om_strongpoint_init(w)`. A space era leaves every table empty (each init checks `om_era_kind`).
- `void om_economy_step(om_world *w, uint64_t tick)`: called once per tick at the end of `om_tactical_step` (`unit.c`), after movement, fire and damage. Order: `om_density_step`, `om_lots_step`, `om_waves_step`, `om_strongpoint_step`, each `(w, tick)`.
- `void om_economy_on_structure_owner(om_world *w, uint32_t structure, int32_t old_owner, int32_t new_owner)`: from `om_structure_set_owner` (`tactical_support.c`) when the owner changes. Fans out to `om_waves_on_structure_owner` (kind 3 capture at the structure's x, y) and `om_strongpoint_on_structure_owner` (mirror owner and state).
- `void om_economy_on_structure_damage(om_world *w, uint32_t structure, double hp_after)`: from `om_structure_apply_damage`. Fans out to `om_strongpoint_on_structure_damage` (state 2 rubble at hp 0).
- `void om_economy_on_area_hit(om_world *w, double x, double y, double radius)`: from `om_unit_fire_aoe_attack` (`unit.c`). Fans out to `om_lots_on_area_hit` (E1d rubble).
- `void om_economy_on_mission_end(om_world *w)`: from `om_mission_end` (`mission.c`), before the debrief. Fans out to `om_waves_on_mission_end` (kind 4).

Per-file entries (declared in the header, defined with empty bodies by K-e2, filled by the piece named):
- `economy_density.c` (E1b): `om_density_init`, `om_density_step`.
- `economy_lots.c` (E1c): `om_lots_init`, `om_lots_step`; E1d also fills `om_lots_on_area_hit` here (the rubble step on lots is lot code; E1d owns `economy_waves.c` and this one function, sequenced after E1c).
- `economy_waves.c` (E1d, new file): `om_waves_init`, `om_waves_step`, `om_waves_log(om_world *w, uint32_t tick, int32_t kind, double x, double y)` (ring: when `n_waves == OM_WAVE_LOG_CAP` the oldest row is dropped), `om_waves_on_structure_owner`, `om_waves_on_mission_end`.
- `economy_place.c` (E1e, new file): `om_economy_place` moves here; K-e2 deletes the stub from `economy_lots.c`.
- `strongpoint.c` (E1h): `om_strongpoint_init`, `om_strongpoint_step`, `om_strongpoint_on_structure_owner`, `om_strongpoint_on_structure_damage`.

**E1i (wiring, widened to `mission.c`, `unit.c`, `tactical_support.c`) lands with K-e2 in the same candidate**, the six calls above plus three rule edits already ruled in the bible:
- R-218-01: in a ground era, `mission.c` caps the structure count at `om_terrain_slope_count(w)` before `om_structure_assign_owners`; `om_strongpoint_init` (E1h) then moves each structure onto its slope (see E1h) and mirrors the table.
- R-218-05: `om_structure_apply_damage` no longer removes a structure at 0 HP: it stays, with HP 0 and its production stopped, and the economy hook marks the strongpoint rubble (state 2). `om_economy_place` at a rubble strongpoint's cell (E1e) refounds it: HP back to 3,000, owner the placer, state 1. `tests/csim/test_E15_S127_taking_a_garrison.py::test_R_127_03_it_takes_damage_like_unit` drops its "never rebuilt" half under ledger row TD-VG-E-3 (rule changed: R-218-05 replaces R-127-03, Westley 2026-10-06).
- R-218-06: `om_mission_compute_starting_force(F)` returns F (the whole force at the start; liberty 2026-10-10 until E-10 S-211 is built: the situation then decides) and `om_mission_compute_reinforcement_capacity` returns 0 (strongpoint production is paid by the holder's economy, R-218-03, not pre-allocated). `tests/csim/test_E15_S126_most_of_my_force_is_out_there.py::test_R_126_04_starting_force_0_20_f` is deleted under ledger row TD-VG-E-2 (rule changed: R-218-06, Westley 2026-10-06).

**Rulings for the builders (all liberties 2026-10-10 unless a rule is cited):**
- E1b density (REPLACED by part c, rev 10): the tick is 20 per second; a ring grows when `tick % 60 == 0`; no new state field (the step reads the tick). Steepness per cell is 1 when a slope segment (`om_terrain_slope_get`) passes within 50 wu of the cell centre, else 0; this replaces the mockup's per-cell scalar. The Q-168-05 base cap is not enforced (flagged in a comment).
- E1c lots: `push/vg-e1c` (007370dc) is the base; `lots_tick` becomes `om_lots_step` (non-static, declared in the header) and `om_lots_init` clears the table.
- E1d waves: E1 logs kinds 3 (capture) and 4 (battle end) only; kinds 0 (shipment), 1 (expansion) and 2 (season) wait for E2 (the behaviours that cause them are E2's; follow-up row). Rubble: a lot is hit when its cell centre lies within `radius` of the hit point; a hit lot steps back one stage per hit (floor stage 0, rubble); a hit lot waits 200 ticks (10 s) before it rises again.
- E1e placement: as the builder drafted (centre cell floor(x/100), floor(y/100); 5x5 footprint checked with `om_terrain_walkable_at` at cell centres; refused on an unwalkable cell or a cell inside an existing lot's footprint; edge-adjacent footprints accepted; the 2-cell ring is a ring only). Refounding a rubble strongpoint (R-218-05) is accepted at its cell regardless of lots.
- E1h strongpoints: the structure slot is the strongpoint id; the best slopes are the longest (ties to the lowest slope index); strongpoint i stands at the midpoint of the i-th best slope and the structure's position is set to that point in `om_strongpoint_init`; owner and state mirror the structure (0 free, 1 held, 2 rubble); production stays the structure code's (R-218-03's economy charge is E2); no drift (R-218-07).
- E1g narrowed: the doctrine list as manifest data only (`doctrines`: ids boom, fortify, press, balanced; the validator requires the four) in `pack_validate.py` and the two manifests; `economy_doctrine.c` is untouched. The unit mix per doctrine waits for Q-179-02 (Westley). The setter, the `doctrine` player command and the unset default (Q-233-01 over the K-e default of balanced) are one later contract piece, K-e3, in E2; the K-e pin of the default moves then under a ledger row. E1g's tests are rewritten to that scope.

Owner rows: `VG-CONTRACT-E2` owns `csrc/economy_internal.h`, `csrc/economy.c`, `csrc/mission.c`, `csrc/unit.c`, `csrc/tactical_support.c` (the E1i lines; depends_on VG-CONTRACT, B9, B10, PP-unit), and `tests/csim/test_VG_E_ke2.py`; VG-E1 drops `csrc/mission.c` and `csrc/unit.c` and keeps the economy files; VG-E1 gains `depends_on` VG-CONTRACT-E2. Reach: every entry above is reached from `om_tactical_step`, `om_mission_setup`, `om_mission_end`, `om_structure_set_owner`, `om_structure_apply_damage` or `om_unit_fire_aoe_attack`, so no `reach_allowlist` entry.

Tests for K-e2 (`tests/csim/test_VG_E_ke2.py`, written first): the entry points exist in the built library with the stated signatures (ctypes lookup on `ouroboros_sim` symbols; internal functions are exported by the shared object); a ground mission's structure count never exceeds the slope count; `om_mission_compute_starting_force(1000.0) == 1000.0`; a structure at 0 HP is still listed; the sorted csim red ids otherwise equal main's.

**K-e2 addendum (2026-10-10, after the audit and the first contract build push/vg-ke2 1c265472):**
- `om_economy_init` is called once at the end of `om_mission_instantiate_map` (`mission.c`), after the terrain is generated and the structures are placed and positioned (the loop in `om_mission_place_structures` sets positions; the init runs after it so `om_strongpoint_init` can move structures onto slopes). The R-218-01 cap stays where `total` is computed.
- `om_economy_step(w, st->current_tick)` runs on every `om_tactical_step` call, before the early return for a unit-less world. The economy does not run during the recon phase (R-092: the clock is stopped), so a test scene that expects growth calls `om_mission_begin` after `om_mission_setup` (TD-VG-E-5).
- `om_economy_on_structure_owner` is called from `om_structure_set_owner` and from `om_structure_on_owner_changed` (a driven capture), only when `old_owner != new_owner`; every hook is a no-op on empty tables (before init).
- `om_economy_on_mission_end` fires once per mission (guarded by `st->end_applied`); the two `om_mission_end` paths share it.
- `om_economy_on_area_hit` takes the target's position (`center->x`, `center->y`) and the attack's radius.
- R-218-05: the structure's `destroyed` flag means rubble (production and capture stop; the strongpoint state is 2). Refounding: `void om_structure_refound(om_world *w, uint32_t structure, int32_t owner)` (declared in `economy_internal.h`, defined in `tactical_support.c` by K-e2) clears `destroyed`, sets HP 3,000 and the owner; `om_economy_place` (E1e) calls it when the footprint's centre cell holds a rubble strongpoint; the placer is `om_player_faction(w)`.
- A ground map with no slopes places no structures and no strongpoints (R-218-01, E-15 "a map with no slope places no strongpoint"). TD-VG-E-5 (test-defect, rule-changed): `tests/csim/test_E01_T3_mission_ground.py::test_structure_slots_are_on_walkable_ground` asserts `placed > 0` only when `om_terrain_slope_count > 0`; `tests/csim/test_VG_E_ke2.py::test_R_218_01_ground_structure_count_at_most_slope_count` drops its `slopes > 0` premise; the E1b, E1c, E1d and E1h scenes call `om_mission_begin` before stepping where they expect the clock to run.
- Owner rows: VG-CONTRACT-E2 does not depend on VG-E1 (the cycle is removed); VG-E1 keeps its dependency on VG-CONTRACT-E2.


**Part a3 rulings (Architect, 2026-10-10, after the part a2 stops):**
- K-e3 (`VG-CONTRACT-E3`): `om_economy_state` gains `double density_economy` and `uint32_t lot_hit_tick[OM_LOT_CAP]`, saved (save 9) and hashed; see `docs/architecture.md`, K-e3. E1b's target reads `density_economy` (set once in `om_density_init` from the player's region economy); E1d's `om_lots_on_area_hit` stamps `lot_hit_tick[i] = tick` when it knocks lot i back one stage (it takes the tick from `w`'s tactical state; the hook signature is unchanged), and `om_waves_step` raises a stage-0 lot to stage 1 when `tick - lot_hit_tick[i] >= 200` and the tick is set; a stage-0 lot with `lot_hit_tick` 0 is a fresh placement and rises by E1c's rule.
- TD-VG-E-6 (test-defect, assumption-changed, E1b): a ground mission always generates terrain in `om_mission_setup`, so `test_no_terrain_no_density_reads_stay_safe` reads before setup (a fresh world: `om_density_at` is 0 everywhere and does not crash) and is renamed `test_before_setup_density_reads_stay_safe`; mission setup derives the terrain seed from the world seed, so `test_follows_terrain_not_rings` becomes: on a scene whose terrain has slopes, two cells at the same ring distance from the core differ when one lies within 50 wu of a slope segment and the other does not (terrain shapes the field); control: on archetype 4 seed 7 (0 slopes) the field after the same steps is 8-fold symmetric about the core cell (amended 2026-10-10: OM_DENSITY_DIAG 0.99 keeps same-ring cells apart). The test engineer verifies both facts on the built tree and, if archetype 4 seed 7 has slopes after all, picks another zero-slope seed and names it. `test_population_does_not_feed_density` is unchanged (K-e3 makes it pass).
- TD-VG-E-7 (test-defect, assumption-changed, E1h): `om_tactical_step` returns before `om_support_tick` on a unit-less world (`csrc/unit.c`), so `test_held_strongpoint_produces_a_unit` advances the clock with `om_tactical_tick_to(w, om_tactical_tick(w) + interval)` (bind both: `om_tactical_tick_to(W, c_uint64) -> c_uint64`, `om_tactical_tick(W) -> c_uint64`) in both loops; `test_save_roundtrip_keeps_table_and_checksum` binds `om_world_checksum_full` as `(W, POINTER(c_uint64), c_uint32) -> c_uint64` and calls it `(w, None, 0)`. `test_rubble_can_be_refounded` is left alone: it goes green once E1e's `om_economy_place` is in the tree.
- TD-VG-E-8 (test-defect, rule-changed, K-e3): the K-e save-pin tests move 8 to 9 and the checksum pins that K-e3's fields move are re-recorded once, old and new named in the row.
- E1g: the tests require every manifest under `assets/eras/*/`, test-only packs included, to carry the doctrines list, so E1g's file list gains `assets/eras/test_era/manifest.json` (VG-E1 owns `assets/eras/*/manifest.json`) and the validator's doctrines check runs for `test_only` packs too (the buildings check keeps its skip).
- TD-VG-E-9 (test-defect, rule-changed, K-e2 puts the economy in the tick): the three "flyer blob trace is unchanged" tests (`test_E01_T2_collision_mask.py`, `test_E01_T2_walker_no_corner_cut.py`, `test_E01_T2_walker_waypoint_arrival.py`) pin `om_world_checksum_full`, which hashes the lot table; lots now appear during the ticks, so the pins move once (old and new named in the row) on the part a3 landing tree. The flyer movement itself is unchanged (the old value returns with the economy step emptied, 2026-10-10 diagnosis). Any other golden that the economy step moves is listed in the same row; a golden that moves for any other reason stops the landing.


### Lane E1 part b: the client layers (Architect rulings, 2026-10-10, after the part b pre-flight)

Part a landed at 8f76be4d and C5 is on main, so part b builds E1k, E1l, E1o and E1p. The pre-flight (read-only scout, 2026-10-10) found six gaps; the rulings below close them. Every number here not taken from the mockup or the bible is a liberty, marked "liberty 2026-10-10" in code.

**K-e4 (`VG-CONTRACT-E4`, the bridge piece, first).** `autoload/c_bridge.gd` gains `send_place(x: float, y: float, building: int) -> bool`, which sends `{"type":"cmd","name":"command","verb":"place","units":[],"x":x,"y":y,"building":building}` on the command path (the K-e frame; `send_unit_command` stays as it is), counts the place as pending, and resolves it from the next command answer: an ack emits `place_result(true, x, y, building)`, an error frame named "command" while a place is pending emits `place_result(false, x, y, building)` (the reason stays in `last_error()`); the signal is `signal place_result(accepted: bool, x: float, y: float, building: int)`. The bridge also gains `strongpoints() -> Array` (the reconstructed view's `strongpoints`, ascending id, each `{id, x, y, owner, state}`), beside `lots()`. Documented in `docs/architecture.md` under the bridge API before implementation; tests in `tests/test_c_bridge_vg_e.gd` (the test is VG-CONTRACT-E1's; K-e4 owns it for this landing, with a ledger note). No pipe or header change.

**Building ids.** The pipe's `building` integer is the position of the building in the era manifest's `buildings` list, in the order written (liberty 2026-10-10; E2 may replace it with a stable id table). The client reads `buildings` from `assets/eras/<era>/manifest.json` the way `autoload/era_config.gd` reads the manifest (E1o parses the file itself; no edit to `era_config.gd`).

**3D hook.** `scenes/ground3d/ground3d.gd` gains one public method, `add_layer(node: Node3D)`, which adds the node inside the SubViewport after `SlopesView` and before `Units3D`, and from then on calls `node.set_terrain(t)` on `set_terrain` and `node.set_frame(frame)` on `set_frame` when the node has those methods. That edit is E1p's (VG-E1 owns `ground3d.gd` for this landing, depends_on VG-C-wire); E1k and E1l are pure `Node3D` scripts with `set_terrain(t)` and `set_frame(frame)` and draw nothing until both have been called.

**E1k `scenes/settlement/settlement_layer.gd` (class `SettlementLayer`, extends Node3D).** Ports the mockup's look onto sim lots: lots come from `frame["lots"]` (the reconstructed view: `{id, x, y, stage, building, owner}`), positioned with `GroundPick.world_to_3d` and `height_at` as `Units3D` does; one `MultiMeshInstance3D` of placeholder boxes, stage 0 (rubble) a flat dark slab, stages 1 to 5 boxes rising with the mockup's `THRESHOLDS` proportions (`settlement_sim.gd:9-35`), jitter and contour yaw per `settlement_mockup.gd` `_flush`/`_lot_yaw` (236-281), owner tint as `StructuresLayer.owner_color`; a lot is a cliff dwelling when the client-side slope at its cell (from the terrain `heights`) exceeds `CLIFF_SLOPE` 0.22 and then shows at most `CLIFF_MAX_STAGE` 3 (both from the mockup). Colours are the approved mockup's (scenes/mockups/settlement/, its stage palette constants, four stage colours and the purple cliff colour, copied by name and value into the paragraph by the test writer at launch and asserted by the look test per stage; natural and placed lots share the stage palette; nothing is drawn in one colour for every lot). [W 2026-10-13: the settlement was rejected as 'very uniform'; the drift audit 2026-10-21 row (a)9 found the game draws every lot in one colour.] Streets: `lots_dump` carries no paths, so the layer builds a hidden route mesh between each stage-2-or-higher lot and its nearest such neighbour within 3 cells (the mockup's `_build_routes` mesh, fed by this neighbour chain; `[DRAFT-e1k-streets]`; `[A 2026-10-13, Architect ruling: :298 wins]`). Stage changes ease as the mockup's `vis()` (dt-based, `RISE_S` 0.9 s per `_process` call; the first frame after load snaps, later stage changes ease; `[P scenes/mockups/settlement/settlement_sim.gd:21, 320-327]`). Exposes `lots_drawn() -> Array` (what is drawn, for qa_state).

**E1l `scenes/settlement/strongpoint_layer.gd` (class `StrongpointLayer`, extends Node3D).** Strongpoints from `frame["strongpoints"]` (`{id, x, y, owner, state}`), one placeholder mesh each (a tall box, 2 cells wide, 3 cells high, liberty 2026-10-10) at its slope position, owner colour as `StructuresLayer.owner_color`, neutral grey when unowned; state rubble (the K-e state value the sim uses for 0 HP) draws a flat dark slab instead. No era asset exists yet, so the era skin of R-218-08 is a follow-up row (FU-E1-strongpoint-skin) and the placeholder is the whole piece. Exposes `strongpoints_drawn() -> Array`.

**E1o `scenes/economy/placement_ui.gd` (class `PlacementUi`, extends Control).** A HUD panel listing the era's buildings by name (the manifest `buildings` entries, index = building id); clicking one enters placement mode and emits `placement_mode_changed(active: bool, building: int)`; a second click or Escape leaves it. While active, the panel draws a footprint ghost: the 5 x 5 cell square (E1e's footprint, 100 wu cells) around the cursor's world point, projected through the host's world-to-screen function the host passes in (`set_projector(callable)`), as a translucent outline; after a `place_result(false, ...)` the ghost flashes red for 1 s (20 frames, liberty) and the panel shows `last_error()`; after `place_result(true, ...)` the mode ends. The panel never sends the command itself: it emits `place_requested(x, y, building)` and E1p calls `CBridge.send_place`. The yield preview is E2.

**E1p (the VG-VIEW link, `scenes/tactical_c/tactical_view.gd` plus the `ground3d.gd` hook).** `_build_shell` adds `SettlementLayer` and `StrongpointLayer` through `_ground3d.add_layer` once the host exists (after `set_terrain`), and `PlacementUi` under `Hud` with a `PANEL_POSITIONS` entry (bottom-left, beside the selection panel; liberty); gives the UI the projector (`_ground3d.to_screen` or the C4 projection the view already uses for `objects_px`); in `_unhandled_input`, a left click while placement mode is active sends `CBridge.send_place(world.x, world.y, building)` via `_to_world` and does not start a selection box; `qa_state` gains top-level `settlement_lots` (the layer's `lots_drawn()`), `strongpoints` (`strongpoints_drawn()`) and `placement` (`{active, building, last_result}`); `_inventory` lists the three nodes. `map_markers` and ticker beats wait for E1m/E1n.

**VG-E1N (lane E1 part b, E1n, S-235 map markers; Tool: Workflow (a client piece that ends in a frame, the S-235 shot of VG-E1W; one file; built on `origin/push/vg-e1n-fix` 87575377 and landed only through the lane E screens landing of `vg-wave13-laneEscreens.js` with VG-E1M and VG-E1W, `args.skip` for tests:n and build:n); owns `scenes/economy/map_markers.gd` (with its `.uid`) and `tests/test_vg_e_e1n.gd`; depends_on VG-E1, VG-A18; Architect rulings 2026-10-14 g6, g7, g9, g10 folded here from the script).** `[W 2026-10-12: Q-235-01 (c)]` `[B R-235-01@E-16:271]` `[B R-235-03@E-16:273]` `[B R-218-05@E-15]` [P scenes/tactical_c/structures_layer.gd:3-7] [P scenes/tactical_c/structures_layer.gd:30] [P scenes/tactical_c/structures_layer.gd:84] [P scenes/tactical_c/tactical_view.gd:1070] [P scenes/tactical_c/tactical_view.gd:1080-1099] [P autoload/c_bridge.gd:509-510] [P autoload/c_bridge.gd:547] [P csrc/pipe/pipe_tactical.c:129] [P csrc/pipe/pipe_tactical.c:169-183] [P csrc/pipe/pipe_tactical.c:234-246] [P csrc/strongpoint.c:18-20] [P csrc/strongpoint.c:68] [P docs/architecture.md:415] The map marks the strongpoints and the side's base of operations (R-235-01); nothing else in R-235-01 is marked here: economic locations and neutral settlements have their own layers (the `lots` rows and `SettlementLayer`, E1p; P6 draws towns), and expansions, forward works and weather (R-235-02) have no sim source, so all of them are stubs of this file: neither drawn nor listed, said in the file header with See also the places lane spec (P6 draws towns through its own layer; MapMarkers is not the P6 path) and E-15 weather; the objective stays with `ObjectiveMarkers`. Relation to E1l: `StrongpointLayer` (E1l, `scenes/settlement/strongpoint_layer.gd`) is the 3D in-world prop for each strongpoint, built from the same `frame["strongpoints"]`, and `StructuresLayer` draws a ring for each strongpoint's structure; `MapMarkers` is the 2D map mark over them, so the marks coexist with the prop and the ring, and the S-235 look verdict judges the diamond as drawn over the structure ring. Interface, fixed in full: `class_name MapMarkers`, `extends Node2D`, node name `MapMarkers`, a 2D shell layer added by VG-E1W through `_piece` after `StructuresLayer`; `func set_frame(frame: Dictionary, local_faction: int, world_scale: float, camera: Vector2) -> void` (the `StructuresLayer.set_frame` signature, `structures_layer.gd:30`, fed by VG-E1W beside the other 2D layer `set_frame` calls with the bridge's reconstructed view, whose `strongpoints` is the last full frame's list sorted by id, `c_bridge.gd:509-510, :547`, so a delta frame keeps it); `func markers() -> Array` of `{kind: String, x: float, y: float, owner: int, state: int}` in world wu, strongpoint rows ascending `id` then the home base, empty before the first `set_frame`, a deep copy. Kind `strongpoint`: one row per `frame["strongpoints"]` entry `{id,x,y,owner,state}` (`pipe_tactical.c:234-246`); `x`, `y` from that entry; `owner` from the joined `frame["structures"]` row of the same `id` (`pipe_tactical.c:129, :169-183`; a strongpoint is a structure, `strongpoint.c:47`) because the strongpoints list is full-frame only and goes stale between full frames (the g2 (b) text of `vg-wave13-laneEscreens.js`: a strongpoint's owner is read from the structures row), falling back to the entry's own `owner` when no structures row exists; `state` is the entry's (0 free, 1 held, 2 rubble, `strongpoint.c:18-20, :68`, R-218-05), and a strongpoint whose joined structures row has `hp` <= 0 reads state 2 (R-218-05: a destroyed strongpoint stays listed as rubble, its row remains at hp 0 and no `structures_removed` is sent, so the entry's own `state` is stale until the next full frame), as does a strongpoint id with no structures row at all, and both are drawn as rubble. Kind `home_base`: the map node of kind `SPAWN_NODE_KIND` 7 (`tactical_view.gd:1070`) nearest the centroid of the local faction's units on the first frame that has own units, read the way `_home_box` reads it (`tactical_view.gd:1080-1099`; the file keeps its own `const SPAWN_NODE_KIND := 7`, a kept copy, See also `tactical_view.gd:1070`); `owner` the local faction, `state` 1; remembered from then on per local faction and recomputed only when `local_faction` changes, so the mark never follows the army onto the enemy's spawn (FINDING-e1n-home-base-follows-army, the `push/vg-e1n-fix` change, pinned by `test_vg_e_e1w.gd` [HOME]); no own units on any frame yet gives no home-base row. Drawing: in `_draw`, each marker at `(world - camera) * world_scale + StructuresLayer.SCREEN_CENTER` (the `StructuresLayer._sync` projection, `structures_layer.gd:84`; no projector path, the same as the 2D map band) in `StructuresLayer.OWN_COLOR`, `ENEMY_COLOR` or `NEUTRAL_COLOR` (owner equal to the local faction, another faction, under 0; `structures_layer.gd:4-6`). The look is a named draft judged by eye at the S-235 shot, never a number this paragraph fixes: `DRAFT-e1n-marker` (ledger row, appended by the landing clerk from the branch's row at the next free id) as built is a diamond outline of radius `MARK_RADIUS_PX` 36 px and width `MARK_WIDTH` 3 px around the structure ring for a strongpoint, a cross of the same size for rubble, a filled 36 px square for the home base; the g7 (a) null-action text (a 24 px ring, a square of the same half-width, a half-size filled square for rubble) is the other candidate, and Westley picks at the shot (question 1). qa_state: this piece adds no key; VG-E1W adds top-level `map_markers` (the `markers()` list) and `_inventory.map_markers` (its count), and the section 2 key and node lists already name them (`:505`, `:507`). Element inventory the S-235 play asserts (VG-E1W's play, `tests/qa/desktop/plays/E/S-235.py`): `map_markers` non-empty with at least one `strongpoint` and one `home_base` row, every row with `owner` and `state` ints, no kind outside the two [R-235-01]; the ticker half is VG-E1M's. Tests first, by a different writer (engineer, `origin/push/vg-e1n-tests` 97fedf68, `tests/test_vg_e_e1n.gd`, line 2 `# NEEDS A DISPLAY`, run under `xvfb-run -a -s '-screen 0 1920x1080x24'`, waiver FU-E1n-pregate-display, ledger `:1334`; red on main for the stated reason "res://scenes/economy/map_markers.gd does not exist", the first failing line to be pasted in the landing report): [CONTROL] (positive control, green on main) `StructuresLayer.set_frame` with two structures keeps one node per structure, `owner_color` maps own and enemy, a structure at the camera projects to the screen centre, and a pixel detector finds its ring; [LOAD] class, name, both methods, empty before a frame; [STRONGPOINT] ids 2, 5, 9 with owners own, enemy, neutral and states 1, 1, 0 give three rows ascending by id, and the stale-list negative control (list says enemy, structures row says own) reads own; [HOME] the kind-7 node nearest the own centroid, one row, after a strongpoint row, recomputed for the other faction; [PROJECT] a marker's drawn position equals the StructuresLayer projection of the same point for two camera and scale values within 1 px; [COLOUR] the painted pixels carry the StructuresLayer colour; [KINDS] no stub kind is ever produced; [DESTROYED] a strongpoint whose structures row has `hp` 0 and whose entry still says state 1 gives state 2 and is drawn as the rubble mark (the case the sim produces), and one whose structures row leaves the frame keeps its row with state 2 likewise. Untested draft behaviour, named so no one reads it as proven: the home-base row's `owner` (the local faction) and `state` 1 values, and the no-home-base-row case when no frame has own units (the test file header says these are not written; the tests piece may add two assertions at the Architect's word). Retired tests: none (no existing test encodes a superseded ruling for this file; the "no edit to events_feed.gd" sentence struck by g2 (b) belongs to VG-E1M). Shared files: none; the ledger row is the one shared-file edit and the clerk re-appends it on landing. Shot: none of its own; a human sees the marks in the S-235 shot (`moment(c, "S-235", "ticker_and_markers")`, camera edge-scrolled so a strongpoint mark is on screen), queued behind S-191 in Westley's folder (g10); the look verdict on that shot is the prototype gate for `DRAFT-e1n-marker`. Rulings logged: DEC-e1n-markers-2026-10-23 in `decisions.md` records g6, g7 (null action), g9 (null action) and g10 so the spec and the log, not only the script, carry them. Stale docs the landing clerk rewrites with the landing: `docs/bible/stories.md:235` S-235 from "not scheduled" to the lane E screens state, and the E-16 S-235 preface gains VG-E1N beside VG-E1M and VG-E1W and the sentence that the marks are partly stubbed (strongpoints and home base live, every other R-235-01 kind a stub). Pre-launch step: the E1n tests' red line ("res://scenes/economy/map_markers.gd does not exist") and the [CONTROL] green are re-observed on the landing base, since the branches are 703 to 867 commits behind main and the figures here are quoted from the branch commit messages; the `.uid` beside `map_markers.gd` is committed with it and is not listed in `owns` (pre_gate NOTE-uid-files, `scripts/specfactory/pre_gate.sh:41-42`: a `.gd.uid` is fine when its `.gd` is added). Reach: `set_frame` is called from the view's frame path by VG-E1W, so the merge gate's wired check passes only when both land together, which is why this piece lands in the lane E screens landing and never alone.

**TD-VG-E-13 (test-defect, assumption-changed, Architect 2026-10-10).** `tests/qa/desktop/plays/E/S-193.py` reads `qa_state["settlement"]["lots"]`; the spec's inventory (section 2) and `S-168.py` use the top-level `settlement_lots` key with lots `{id, x, y, stage, building, owner}`. The play moves to `settlement_lots`; its stage range 0 to 5 and "max stage at least 2" are unchanged.

**Pieces.** VG-CONTRACT-E4 (owns `autoload/c_bridge.gd`, `tests/test_c_bridge_vg_e.gd`; depends_on VG-CONTRACT-E1, VG-CONTRACT; in flight, lands with part b). VG-E1 gains `scenes/tactical_c/tactical_view.gd` and `scenes/ground3d/ground3d.gd` for the E1p link (depends_on VG-C6b, VG-C-wire added). Tests per piece: `tests/test_vg_e_e1k.gd`, `test_vg_e_e1l.gd`, `test_vg_e_e1o.gd`, `test_vg_e_e1p.gd` (each with a positive control; the layer tests feed a hand-built frame and terrain and read `lots_drawn()` / `strongpoints_drawn()` and the MultiMesh instance counts; the UI test feeds a fake manifest and projector; the E1p test instantiates the view with a fixture terrain and checks the inventory keys and the click routing through a stub bridge). Plays: `S-168.py`, `S-193.py` (after TD-VG-E-13), `s218.py` on the landing; S-237 is a look review for Westley from the play shots.

**Part b under method rev 2 (rev 9, 2026-10-10; script `workflows_2026-10-07/vg-wave5-laneE1b-r2.js`, the rev 8 script `vg-wave5-laneE1b.js` is superseded).** The lane's first landing is a skeleton, seen before anything fans out:
- **E1k-skel** (`scenes/settlement/settlement_layer.gd`, the file E1k fills later): `set_terrain(t)`, `set_frame(frame)`, one `MultiMeshInstance3D` box per lot of `frame["lots"]` at `GroundPick.world_to_3d` and `height_at` as `Units3D` does, every box the same size and the owner tint, `lots_drawn()`. No stages, cliffs, streets or easing: those are E1k, same file, after the verdict. The `tests/test_vg_e_e1k.gd` file lands with it; its id and instance-count assertions are green on the skeleton, the stage, cliff and street assertions stay red until E1k (tests first, as every piece).
- **E1p-a** (`scenes/ground3d/ground3d.gd` gains `add_layer(node)` as the "3D hook" paragraph says; `scenes/tactical_c/tactical_view.gd` `_build_shell` adds `SettlementLayer` through it after `set_terrain`, `qa_state` gains `settlement_lots`, `_inventory` lists the node). No `PlacementUi`, no `StrongpointLayer`, no click routing: those are **E1p-b**, same two files, after the verdict. `tests/test_vg_e_e1p.gd` lands with E1p-a; its inventory, `settlement_lots` and `add_layer` order assertions are green, the click assertions stay red until E1p-b.
- **K-e4** builds beside E1k-skel (disjoint file) and lands in the same batch.
- The skeleton landing runs `tests/qa/desktop/plays/E/S-193.py` under xvfb (vulkan renderer) on the merged tree, in the ground era (`--era greek_placeholder`: lots exist only under `Ground3D`, E1p-a above) with the camera edge-scrolled onto the first lot before the shot (TD-VG-E-14, fix piece VG-E1-SHOT after the 2026-10-10 1927 shot showed the space fallback); the clerk copies its shot and qa_state into `docs/northstar/story_reviews/shots/<run id>/` and adds the row to `story_reviews/E.md` (story S-237, reviewer verdict from a Sonnet look, Westley pending). That row is "Now" item 6; the fan-out stage starts only when it reads approved, or with the fix piece the Architect writes when it reads rejected.
- Fan-out (stage `fanout`, after approval): E1k (full), E1l, E1o in parallel; E1p-b after them; one landing; the three E plays and `story_reviews/E.md` rows per piece (S-193 for E1k, s218 for E1l, S-168 is E1r's play first (the placement clear is the first thing it must show); E1o and E1p-b depend on VG-E1R and take `plays/E/S-168.py` over after E1R attaches, Architect message to the Dispatcher, 2026-10-13).
- Before any builder starts, the five test writers re-run on the built tree (`rules.md` section (d)): each merges `origin/main` (the part b records are on it) and, for E1p, confirms that instantiating the view with the fixture terrain passes today as its positive control, then dumps the qa_state keys one real run returns; a premise that fails is fixed on the test branch and reported, never left for a builder to find.
- A builder that meets a test it believes wrong stops with `stop_reason` starting `test-defect:`; the script's ruler (`rules.md` section (e)) rules it or returns a brief for the Architect. Every builder and the clerk run `scripts/specfactory/pre_gate.sh` (until it lands: `merge_gate.sh --revert` plus the noise check by hand) before a push and before the lock.

### Lane E1 part c: the settlement port (rev 10, Architect 2026-10-10, after Westley rejected the S-237 skeleton shot; rev 10e after four fresh audits)

**Why.** Westley rejected the first settlement shot ("far too circular"): the settlement engine is a sister system of the landscape generator, stochastic and density-field driven (Q-166-02, R-166-01, R-166-02, R-193-01 "never form rings"). The settlement audit of 2026-10-10 found that the sim's lot layout (a fixed 10 x 10 block at the canvas centre, `csrc/economy_lots.c` `LOT_BLOCK`) and its ring field (`csrc/economy_density.c`, slope mask only, no height or water) were a builder draft (007370dc) written into spec rev 8 by the Architect; no Westley decision asked for either. Westley 2026-10-10: port the approved mockup faithfully first, prototype the banded contour look he showed second, skeleton shot before any fan-out. The E1b and E1c bullets in part a2 above (200 wu cells, the slope mask, the block) describe what landed and is replaced here; E1d, E1e, E1g, E1h and the K-e contracts stand. The E1 fan-out (E1k full, E1l, E1o, E1p-b) waits on this part's shot.

**Source of every number.** The approved mockup `scenes/mockups/settlement/settlement_sim.gd` (S) and its field map `scenes/mockups/landscape/field_map.gd` (FM), read at ff2ed55f. Westley approved the mockup 2026-10-06; its constants are his approved look. A number not in S or FM is a named draft, listed at the end.

**K-e5 (`VG-CONTRACT-E5`, contract piece, lands first, second-Fable audit; depends_on VG-CONTRACT-E3).** Save format 9 to 10 and the density and lots paragraphs of `docs/architecture.md` rewritten before any code. Saved economy block (today's order, `save.c:425-450`: `n_lots`, lot rows, `density_present`, the D grid, strongpoints, doctrine, waves, `density_economy`, `lot_hit_tick`), amended: after `density_present`, and only when it is 1: `uint32 density_side` (cells per side, from `om_terrain_cells`, at most `OM_DENSITY_MAX_SIDE` 240), `D[side*side]` doubles, `P[side*side]` doubles (both Q16-truncated), `uint32 n_active`, `uint32 active[n_active]` (the growth frontier in order), `uint32 core_cell`; then strongpoints, doctrine and waves unchanged; then `density_economy`, `lot_hit_tick[OM_LOT_CAP]`, and `uint32 next_lot_id` (always written; 1 on the K-e5 tree where the old code still numbers lots `n_lots + 1`, E1q switches both creators to the counter). The checksum hashes the same fields in the same order. Load rejects `density_side > OM_DENSITY_MAX_SIDE`, `n_active > side * side`, any `active[k] >= side * side`, `core_cell >= side * side`; after the terrain slot loads, `om_density_rebuild_inputs` runs only when `apply` is set; in E1q it rebuilds the derived grids and clears the field (`density_present = 0`) when no terrain is present or its cell dimensions differ from `density_side`; on the K-e5 tree it is a no-op, so a load behaves exactly as today (growth continues on the legacy 120 grid). `OM_LOT_CAP` 2048 (`DRAFT-lot-cap`). So the K-e5 tree still compiles and runs the old growth until E1q lands: `OM_DENSITY_SIDE` 120 stays untouched and marked legacy (its canvas and core derivations in `economy_density.c:20-35` keep `test_VG_E_e1b.py` green on the K-e5 tree), `OM_DENSITY_MAX_SIDE` 240 is added and `D[]`, `P[]`, `active[]` are sized to its square, the old init sets `density_side = 120`, `n_active = 0`, `core_cell = 60 * 120 + 60`, save and checksum loop over `density_side`, and `om_density_rebuild_inputs(w)` is declared in `csrc/economy_internal.h` as a no-op stub that E1q fills; K-e5 also declares the derived grids E1q fills, in `om_economy_state`, unsaved and unhashed, sized to `OM_DENSITY_MAX_SIDE` squared: `int32_t elev_cm[]`, `uint8_t water[]`, `uint8_t wdist[]`, `uint16_t slope_q[]`, `uint8_t lane[]`, `int32_t lot_at[]`; E1q deletes the legacy define. One test hook, the one new public symbol (header, `docs/northstar/spec_factory/signatures.toml` with epics, confidence and note, on the header's hard-fixture path; `OM_ABI_VERSION` 6 to 7 as K-e did for added symbols), documented before use: `int om_terrain_set_heights(om_world *w, const int32_t *cm, uint32_t cells_w, uint32_t cells_h)` replaces the height grid of a generated ground mission with the given cm values (dimensions must match, else 0; returns 0 with no terrain), then computes the five band floors as generation does (`terrain.c:737-749`: `lo` and `hi` over the cells above `TG_DRY_LEVEL_WU`, `step = (hi - lo) / TG_BAND_DIV`; when no cell is above the dry level the hook takes `lo` and `hi` as the minimum and maximum of all cells, so the scan never overflows) and bands the cells with a new shared helper `ts_bands_from_floors(const int32_t *h, size_t n, const int32_t floors[5], uint8_t *band)` extracted from the band loop at `terrain.c:750-753` and used by `ts_save_read:1190-1193` too (so a save round trip on synthetic terrain matches), then `om_terrain_apply_ground` (`terrain.c:1082`: walkable, slopes, walk masks and the mapgen blocked bits rewritten (blocked rects laid after generation are erased; obstacle discs and cover rows survive, said in the header), move epoch bumped), then `om_density_rebuild_inputs(w)`; returns 1. Flat test terrains use heights above 6000 cm (the dry test is strict). `scenes/load_game/load_game.gd:5` `CURRENT_SAVE_VERSION` goes to 10 with the sidecar. Lot stages in the beta: 0 rubble (E1d), 1 staked, 2 scaffold, 3 small, 4 large (Q-193-02's five states; "empty" is no lot); stage 5 reserved; `lots_dump` and `lots_changed` row shape unchanged. Sidecar: `csrc/pipe/pipe_internal.h` `OM_PIPE_SAVE_FORMAT_VERSION` goes to 10 (it is 8 today; K-e3 missed it: finding row `FINDING-pipe-save-version`). Owns `csrc/save.c`, `csrc/checksum_full.c`, `csrc/om_internal.h`, `csrc/economy_internal.h`, `csrc/economy_density.c` (the stub, the fields and the sizing only; E1q rewrites it after), `csrc/pipe/pipe_internal.h`, `csrc/ouroboros_sim.h`, `docs/northstar/spec_factory/signatures.toml`, `csrc/terrain.c` (the hook and the band helper), `scenes/load_game/load_game.gd`, `scripts/specfactory/reach_allowlist.toml` (the hook is test-only, listed there), `docs/architecture.md` (density, lots, stages 1 to 4, save format 10, ABI 7). The K-e5 test file binds the hook through ctypes itself (no `conftest.py` edit). Goldens: the F1, F2, F3, B3_C2 and PP goldens hash `om_world_checksum` (`sim.c:601-618`, regions and tick) and must stay green, the Goldens step stops the lane if any moves; the three flyer-blob pins and W7 `PINNED_FULL` hash `om_world_checksum_full` and move with the economy block, handled by TD-VG-E-17, which also narrows the flyer pins to the units and movement sections so later economy changes leave them still. A Goldens step after the K-e5 build (one engineer) verifies this. Tests pinned to format 9 or ABI 6 move under one ledger row `TD-VG-E-16` (old 9 and 6, new 10 and 7): `tests/csim/test_VG_E_ke3.py::test_ke3_save_format_version_is_9`, `tests/csim/test_E01_T3_contract.py` (the round-trip pin at :164), `tests/csim/test_VG_E_contract.py::test_save_round_trip_at_format_9_keeps_empty_new_state`, `tests/test_load_game_wrong_version.gd:14`, `tests/csim/test_E16_B5_save_version.py:97` (its "later build, refused" version 10 becomes 11), and the C2 save pin; no `om_world_checksum` golden moves (regions and tick only; the economy block is hashed only by `om_world_checksum_full`, `checksum_full.c:446`, used by lockstep, `pipe_net.c:85`, and `tactical_trace`): the Goldens step verifies that the F1, F2, F3, B3_C2, PP and parity goldens stay green on the K-e5 build and stops if any moves; the ABI pins move under the same row: `tests/csim/conftest.py:64`, `tests/specfactory/test_pp_contract.py:29`, `tests/csim/test_E02_S020_abi.py:22`, `tests/csim/test_VG_E_contract.py:70-74`, `tests/transport/test_pipe_vg_e.py:189`, `tests/transport/test_pipe_shapes.py:80`, `docs/northstar/spec_factory/signatures.toml:8`, `docs/architecture.md:215-217, 396`; the stale version comment at `checksum_full.c:34` is rewritten. The hook is the only header change.

**E1q-field (`csrc/economy_density.c`, rewritten in place; public reads `om_density_at`, `om_density_init` unchanged; owner VG-E1).** State in `om_economy_state`: the saved fields above plus derived grids rebuilt by `om_density_rebuild_inputs(w)` at init and at the end of load (after `sv_parse_slots`, since terrain loads after the economy block): `int32 elev_cm[]` from one `om_terrain_get_heights` call, `uint8 water[]` (1 when `elev_cm < 0`; the sim has no lake or river mask, follow-up row `FU-E1-water-mask` for when the terrain lane adds one), `uint8 wdist[]` (Chebyshev distance to the nearest water cell by 8-neighbour BFS to depth `WDIST_CAP` 8; cells farther than that keep `WDIST_NONE` 255 as the mockup does, S:150, 156), `uint16 slope_q[]` (mean |dh| over the orthogonal neighbours that exist (fewer than `N_ORTHO` at the map edge, FM:392-400) in wu / `OM_TERRAIN_CELL_WU` / `SLOPE_NORM` 6.25, clamped to 1, stored Q16; FM:380-402; 6.25 is FM's `PEAK_HEIGHT_WU * 0.25 / (SPACING * 2)`), `uint8 lane[]` (1 when `gx % LANE_PERIOD == LANE_PHASE || gy % LANE_PERIOD == LANE_PHASE`, `LANE_PERIOD` 4, `LANE_PHASE` 3, S:89), `int32 lot_at[]` (lot index per cell, -1 none; E1q-field rebuilds it from the lot table in `om_density_rebuild_inputs`; E1q-lots sets the cell when it creates a lot). If `om_terrain_get_heights` returns 0 or the objective node is missing, `density_present` stays 0 and the mission has no settlement (every density read returns 0.0, as the pre-setup test already checks). The core is the mission objective (`om_objective_get_center(w, 0)`, read once at init and saved as `core_cell`; Q-193-07 below); init sets `P[core] = 1`, `active = {core}`, `density_side` from `om_terrain_cells`. Named literals for the constants lint: `CM_PER_WU` 100, `N_ORTHO` 4, `N_DIRS` 8, `WDIST_NONE` 255. W7 `PINNED_FULL` (`tests/csim/test_PP_portability.py`) moves again at E1q (the economy block changes with the new growth) and gets its own re-record row then; the flyer pins, now units and movement sections (TD-VG-E-17), stay.

Growth every `OM_DENSITY_RING_TICKS` 60 ticks (3 s, S:10), ported in place exactly as the mockup (S:210-236; Godot dictionaries keep insertion order, so the mockup is deterministic and the port keeps its order): walk `active` in stored order; for each i and each of the 8 in-map neighbours j in the mockup's loop order `dy` then `dx` over -1..1, that is NW, N, NE, W, E, SW, S, SE (S:214-216), `v = P[i] * factor(i, j)`; when `v > P[j] + P_EPS` (0.0005) and `v > P_MIN` (0.02): `P[j] = v` at once (in place, so j can pass it on in the same step), and j is appended to `next` the first time it improves this step (a per-step `uint8 queued[]` flag). Then `active = next`. `factor` (S:180-191): `f = BASE_DECAY` 0.99, `f *= DIAG_DECAY` 0.99 on a diagonal; if `water[j]`: return `f * WATER_CROSS` 0.85; `f *= 1 - min(|elev_j - elev_i| / CM_PER_WU / CLIMB_WU, CLIMB_CLAMP) * CLIMB_HALF` with `CLIMB_WU` 900 (wu; `elev` is cm), `CLIMB_CLAMP` 0.8, `CLIMB_HALF` 0.5; `f *= 1 - STEEP_W * slope_j`, `STEEP_W` 0.25; if `wdist[j] > WATER_NEAR_CELLS` (3): `f *= NONWATER_FAR` 0.99. Then for every cell with `P > 0` in row-major order: `mat = M_START 0.6 + 0.4 * min(1, tick / M_FULL)`, `M_FULL` 6000 ticks (300 s, S:194); `tgt = min(1, P * mat * econ)`, `econ` as today (K-e3, Q-222-01 (a): `density_economy / OM_FIELD_MAX` clamped to [0, 1], sampled once at init); if `tgt > D`: `D += (tgt - D) * EASE` 0.35. At the end of every growth step `D` and `P` are truncated through `q16()` for every cell with `P > 0` (so a save and load mid-growth continues as an uninterrupted run would). The mockup holds `P` and `D` as float32; the port uses doubles, a deliberate departure. The econ term is not in the mockup; it is Westley's Q-222-01 answer and stays; a region at economy 0 grows no town (S-115). Plain double arithmetic, `fabs` and `fmin` only; every constant a `#define`. Space eras: `density_present` 0, all reads 0.0. `csrc/economy_waves.c:43` (battle-end wave at the canvas centre, today `OM_DENSITY_SIDE * OM_TERRAIN_CELL_WU` = 12000) reads half the map canvas from `om_terrain_cells` (`w_cells * OM_TERRAIN_CELL_WU / 2`, `h_cells * OM_TERRAIN_CELL_WU / 2`; when it returns 0, no terrain, the old constant centre 12000 stays as the fallback), never `density_side` (VG-E1 gains the file with depends_on VG-CONTRACT-E2). Cost: the active ring plus one pass over the grid per 60 ticks; a full 24000 canvas is 57,600 cells; `DRAFT-density-budget` 2 ms per growth step, printed by a timing control, not asserted.

**E1q-lots (`csrc/economy_lots.c` rewritten in place, plus two lines in `csrc/economy_place.c`; reads `om_lot_count`, `om_lot_get_at`, the `lots_dump` writer unchanged; owner VG-E1).** No block, no appear timer (`LOT_APPEAR_TICKS` retired). Every `LOT_RISE_TICKS` 20 ticks (the existing value, 1 s; the mockup's `RISE_S` 0.9 is an animation time, so this is a named draft `DRAFT-lot-rise-ticks`): scan cells by Chebyshev ring from the core, ring 0 outward, row-major within a ring (deterministic and derivable, no saved order); a cell is buildable when `water == 0 && wdist > SHORE_MARGIN (1) && lane == 0 && !footprint_blocked(cell)` (S:176-177; `path` is E2; `footprint_blocked` is true inside the 5 x 5 footprint of any lot with `owner >= 0`, derived, not saved); a buildable cell with `lot_at == -1` and `D >= THRESHOLD_1` 0.10 gets a new lot at stage 1 at its centre `((gx + 0.5) * 100, (gy + 0.5) * 100)` with `id = next_lot_id++` (`next_lot_id` starts at 1 in `om_lots_init`, saved; `economy_place.c:51` uses the same counter instead of `n_lots`, which today duplicates an id); every eligible cell gets its lot in the same scan; at `OM_LOT_CAP` no lot is created (the cap test reads `om_lot_count() == OM_LOT_CAP` and an eligible reached cell without a lot). Each lot's target stage is the count of thresholds its cell's `D` has passed (0.10, 0.22, 0.38, 0.58; S:9), at most `CLIFF_MAX_STAGE` 3 when `slope > CLIFF_SLOPE` 0.22 (S:239-246); a lot rises one stage per lot step toward its target and never falls except to rubble (E1d). Placement (E1e) keeps its 5 x 5 footprint and its overlap refusal; the R-193-03 clearing and refill stay E2 with drift check e item 1's note, so a solo commander's production placement (Westley 2026-10-10) lands in E2 on this grid. Known cost: `lots_changed` in `pipe_tactical.c:262-275` compares O(n²) per frame; at the cap that is about 4M compares, follow-up row `FU-E1-lots-delta-index` (E2 or sooner if the playthrough gate shows it).

**E1q-shot (no new client code).** `tests/qa/desktop/plays/E/S-193.py` as fixed on `push/vg-e1-shot` (ground era, 600 ticks, camera scrolled to the first lot) runs on the merged tree; the test writer dumps its premises first (rules.md (d)): the mission's region economy (if it is 0 the play picks a mission whose region economy is at least `OM_FIELD_MAX * 0.4`, as TD-VG-E-10 did for the tests, and says so). The clerk queues the shot as the new S-237 row. What Westley should see: boxes spreading from the objective along the terrain, thinner uphill and across steep ground, none in the sea, a gap every fourth row and column, denser near the core. The claims about uphill and steep ground are untested on sim heights (the sim adds 80 wu detail noise the mockup's smoothed heights lacked); if the shot shows the noise dominating, that is a finding for the next prototype, not a reason to tune constants without Westley.

**Tests (fenced, written first on the K-e5 tree, rules.md (d); owner VG-E1's glob `tests/csim/test_VG_E_*.py`).** New `tests/csim/test_VG_E_e1q_field.py`: positive control, `density_side == canvas / 100` on a ground mission; on a synthetic flat terrain with no sea (all heights equal, set through `om_terrain_set_heights` on a generated ground mission) the reach after k growth steps is at least k cells and the field is 8-fold symmetric about the core (transposition exact; the other seven within `DRAFT-symmetry-ulps` 4 Q16 units); the same seed hashes the same across two processes; a synthetic terrain with a sea strip has lower `D` just across it than on the near shore at equal ring (the mockup and the port carry `P` and `D` through water at `WATER_CROSS`; no lot stands there, proved in the lots file); a 900 wu ridge gives lower `D` behind it than the flat control; `econ` 0 gives `D == 0` everywhere; a save at step 5 then 5 more steps equals 10 uninterrupted steps (the frontier round trip); a timing control prints the growth-step cost. New `tests/csim/test_VG_E_e1q_lots.py`: a lot exists on every buildable cell with `D >= 0.10` and nowhere `D < 0.10`; none on a lane cell, in the sea, or within one cell of it; stage equals the threshold count and never exceeds 3 where slope > 0.22; ids strictly ascend, survive a save round trip, and a placed lot's id is unique; the lot count on a flat open canvas after 1200 ticks exceeds 36 (the old block's ceiling); with `OM_LOT_CAP` reached (a test build with the cap lowered through its define is not allowed; the test writer fills the cap on a large flat canvas or marks the case parked) no further lot appears on an eligible reached cell. Retired or moved under ledger rows (test-defect, assumption-changed, Architect 2026-10-10): `TD-VG-E-18` `test_VG_E_e1b.py::test_follows_terrain_not_rings` (the symmetry control encoded ring growth on 200 wu cells on a real terrain; the flat synthetic control above replaces it) and `::test_one_ring_per_tick` (ring unit 200 wu and one ring per step; in-place propagation can exceed one ring; becomes "at least k"); `TD-VG-E-19` `test_VG_E_e1c.py::test_lot_records_are_well_formed` (one lot per 200 wu cell) and `::test_streets_are_left_open` (open 200 wu cells; now 100 wu lane cells, same connectivity meaning) and its `stage_of` helper (1 + count becomes count); `TD-VG-E-20` `test_VG_E_e1d.py` `mission()` (:80-90) gains the premises a lot needs (as `test_VG_E_e1c.py:60-77`: era kind 1, a home region of the player faction with `economy=40`, walking movement kinds for types 0 to 5; TD-VG-E-10's reason: a space era or a region at economy 0 grows nothing); the e1d scene keeps its own `faction_id=1`, `military=500` and `om_mission_setup(w, 0, 0, 0)` unless the test writer's premise dump shows a lot cannot rise under them, in which case the row says what changed and why; assertions unchanged. `test_VG_E_e1b.py`'s remaining tests sample the field at 200 wu centres through `om_density_at` and keep their meaning. `tests/test_vg_e_e1k.gd` is client-only and unchanged (its stage 5 case feeds a fake frame). The W7 re-record (test_PP_portability.py PINNED_FULL) is TD-VG-E-21, done by the landing clerk on the joined tree; ruler rows take the next free number after TD-VG-E-21.

**Open for Westley, defaults named.** `Q-193-07` (register row added with this spec) the core: (a) the mission objective (default; one call; R-193-01 "the core's position"); (b) the mockup's site search (water near, flat, mid-height, away from the edge) over sim terrain; (c) the local faction's base. Named drafts: `DRAFT-lot-cap` 2048, `DRAFT-density-budget` 2 ms. The banded noise-contour look (his two reference images, 2026-10-10) is a prototype in the mockup scene first (prototype gate), a separate brief, not this part.

**Pieces and owners.** `VG-CONTRACT-E5` (new row; owns the files above; depends_on VG-CONTRACT-E3, VG-CONTRACT-E2, TERRAIN-3 for `terrain.c`). The port is VG-E1's own work (VG-E1 is in flight and owns `economy_density.c`, `economy_lots.c` and the `test_VG_E_*.py` glob; it gains `economy_place.c` and `economy_waves.c`, owned today by VG-CONTRACT-E2, attached, with depends_on VG-CONTRACT-E2 and VG-CONTRACT-E5); pieces E1q-field and E1q-lots are VG-E1 wave 9. A record clerk adds the rows (`VG-CONTRACT-E5`, the VG-E1 owns and depends_on, ledger rows `FINDING-pipe-save-version`, `FU-E1-water-mask`, `FU-E1-lots-delta-index`, `DRAFT-lot-cap`, `DRAFT-density-budget`) before the first launch; `pieces.py --check` clean is a launch precondition.
