**VG-B25-vast-space (lane B, after B24; owner `scenes/galaxy/ambient_rig.gd`, its three new shader files, one `qa_state` line in `scenes/galaxy/galaxy_view.gd`, `tests/test_vg_b_b25.gd`, `tests/galaxy_field/b25_gpu_checks.gd` and `.tscn`).** `[W 2026-10-02: "vast space gets the dim wash, real-depth star layers and fading edges together"]` `[P docs/northstar/plans/2026-10-02-galaxy-bubble-field-spec.md Step 5]` Never built (scout 2026-10-12: no wash, depth layer or wrapping dust on main or on origin/push/galaxy-field). Step 5's text is the spec, copied here: a very dim direction-only wash computed once; four static star layers, one draw each, at 50, 125, 400 and 1,250 units (the 0.5 scale of Step 5's 100, 250, 800, 2,500), world-fixed shells with Gaussian thickness R/4 clipped at 2R, star size growing with depth so stars stay 1 to 3 px, far plane 3,000 (DRAFT-b25-far; covers the 1,250 layer and its 2R clip at 2,500); the rig's old values become `DRAFT-b25-*` rows with the old value beside each `[A 2026-10-12, drafter gap ruling]`; density and brightness fall off smoothly in every layer, nothing stops at a radius; one wrapping volume of motes centred on the camera, fading near its faces, replacing the radius-60 dust; distant galaxy impostors and the soft zoom limit stay out. ### Rev 12e (2026-10-12, Architect restart): the gate's user directory, and the wave-12 numbers the drafters corrected

**HARNESS-userdir (lane H; owner `scripts/specfactory/merge_gate.sh`, `scripts/run_godot_tests.sh`, `tests/qa/desktop/daemon_client.py`, `tests/specfactory/test_gate_userdir.py`; after the B23 landing, in the record batch's wave; the row depends on `VG-GUARD`, which owns the first and third file until it is attached).** `[P project.godot:15-16: use_custom_user_dir=true, custom_user_dir_name="data"]` `[P tests/qa/desktop/daemon_client.py:28: the driver reads the user dir from XDG_DATA_HOME or ~/.local/share, and only to clear the privacy flag]` `[P scripts/run_godot_tests.sh:42-46: OM_SAVE_DIR gets a temp dir per run; nothing does the same for the user dir]` `[P autoload/ui_data_bus.gd:12: SETTINGS_PATH user://settings.cfg; the saved era_id is applied at boot]` The B23 gate of 2026-10-12 read Westley's own `~/.local/share/data/settings.cfg` (era_id greek_placeholder, written 18:22) and two client tests went red on his setting, not on the code: finding `FINDING-ambient-user-dir`. Rule: a gate, a test run and a desktop play never read or write the machine's user directory. `merge_gate.sh` exports `XDG_DATA_HOME="$SCRATCH_ROOT/xdg"` once, before the first Godot stage, so every stage it runs (import, acceptance_godot, playthrough, lane_plays, look) boots on an empty user dir that dies with `SCRATCH_ROOT`. `run_godot_tests.sh` mirrors its OM_SAVE_DIR block: when `XDG_DATA_HOME` is unset it exports a `mktemp -d` dir and removes it in `cleanup`; when set, it is left alone (the gate's value wins). `daemon_client.py` does the same, fixed when the `GodotGame` is constructed: `godot_env` returns `XDG_DATA_HOME` as `<scratch>/xdg` unless the caller's environment has a non-empty one, and `GodotGame.__init__` computes the instance's `user_dir` (`<XDG_DATA_HOME>/data`) once from that env and hands the same dir to its `DaemonClient`; nothing is computed at `start()` or at import, and `write_privacy_flag` is an instance method that follows it (Architect 2026-10-13; `test_galaxy_quality.py:51-58` already sets its own and is the pattern). A play that needs a settings value writes it into that dir before `start()`, never into `~`. Tests (an engineer, red first on the built tree): (1) `merge_gate.sh` contains the export line before the first `GODOT_BIN` use, by text, with the variable name; (2) `run_godot_tests.sh` run with `XDG_DATA_HOME` unset on a one-line test script leaves `~/.local/share/data` untouched (mtime and listing equal before and after) and the script's environment names an `XDG_DATA_HOME` under the temp root; (3) `daemon_client.py` `godot_env(base_env: dict, scratch: Path, save_dir: Path | None) -> dict` (a module function returning the whole child env, used by both start paths, so the test needs no Godot) returns an `XDG_DATA_HOME` under the scratch when the caller's environment has none and returns the caller's value when it has one; positive control for each: the pre-piece file text fails (1), a stub that skips the export fails (2) and (3). No known-red rows for the two tests this finding explained (`test_3e_control_space_era_has_no_ground_layer`, `test_c5_4_move_order_single_command_and_units_move`); if either stays red on an empty user dir, that is a new finding, not this one. `FINDING-ambient-user-dir` closes with the landing sha. Architect 2026-10-12: merge_gate.sh exports XDG_DATA_HOME=$SCRATCH_ROOT/xdg with $SCRATCH_ROOT/xdg/godot/export_templates symlinked to ~/.local/share/godot/export_templates and nothing else (never the data/ or app_userdir subtree), so the ambient settings.cfg is hidden while headless exports still build; acceptance = test_headless_linux_export_builds and test_windows_export_builds_headless green under the gate env, plus a positive control that a planted data/settings.cfg in the real home does not change qa_state() era. The acceptance list cites tests/transport/test_fetch_godotsteam.py and tests/transport/test_vg_f_w8d_presets.py (not owned by this piece).

**HARNESS-csim-xdist (lane H; Tool: Workflow (harness piece); owner `tests/csim/conftest.py` (and `tests/transport/conftest.py` if one exists or is needed), `scripts/specfactory/merge_gate.sh` (the csim and transport stage invocations only; the piece owns those lines), `scripts/specfactory/merge_gate_support.py` (ordered after HARNESS-gate-keep-red-evidence, already in depends_on), `scripts/specfactory/requirements.txt` (the line `pytest-xdist>=3.6,<4`; the gate's venv gains it), `tests/specfactory/test_csim_xdist.py`; depends_on HARNESS-gate-base-after-lock, HARNESS-provenance-scope, VG-E-prodslot, HARNESS-gate-keep-red-evidence (prodslot owns `tests/csim/conftest.py` first; keep-red edits the same `merge_gate.sh` statements first)).** [P tests/csim/conftest.py:41] [P tests/csim/conftest.py:22] Today `tests/csim/conftest.py` runs `make` in the repo root when the conftest is imported (the module-level `_build_libs()` call, the make call at :41) and hardcodes `LIB_PATH` (:22) and `DBG_LIB_PATH` (:23); under xdist every worker imports the conftest, so every worker would run make at once in one tree (a race on the .so and on the object files) and all would load the same path. Change: the import-time build is guarded and skipped when `OM_CSIM_LIB` is set, and the controller (a `pytest_configure` hook that runs when `worker_id` is absent and xdist really distributes) copies both built libraries to the directory `$TMPDIR/om_csim_<pid>` (the main process id; removed in `pytest_unconfigure`) and exports `OM_CSIM_LIB` and `OM_CSIM_LIB_DBG`; the hook calls no second make, so the build is the import-time one; a build failure reaches the workers through `OM_CSIM_BUILD_ERROR`. Workers inherit the environment, do not run the conftest's make, and load the two exported paths; `test_PP_move_collision_a5.py:163-164` still runs `make` for `build/O0` and `build/O2` (separate targets, one worker under loadfile) and `test_PP_portability.py:147` runs a dry-run make; the two E17 S186 files read the repo-root `.so`, which stays in place; `LIB_PATH` and `DBG_LIB_PATH` read their variable when set, else today's path (so a bare `pytest tests/csim`, `-p no:xdist` and `-n 0` are unchanged). `merge_gate_support.py regression` gains `--pytest-extra '<args>'` (split with shlex, passed as `extra_args` to both `run_pytest` calls at mgs.py:697 and :700, and appended to `suite_key` so a cached baseline from a different flag set is a miss); mg.sh:333 passes `--pytest-extra '-n 5 --dist loadfile'` (DRAFT-xdist-workers) and the transport call `--pytest-extra '-n 2'`; positive control: the argv built by `run_pytest` contains `-n 5 --dist loadfile` after `no:cacheprovider`. The gate's csim stage runs `python -m pytest tests/csim -n <DRAFT-xdist-workers 5> --dist loadfile -p no:cacheprovider -q` (loadfile keeps each file's tests on one worker, so module-scoped fixtures and the OM_SCALE gating keep their meaning; the worker count is the named draft [DRAFT-xdist-workers]; three stacked gates of 5 workers plus 4 lab workers are 19 workers on 16 cores, so no claim is made that they fit, and the full gates are serialised by the flock, so the overshoot probably does not bite); the transport stage the same with `-n 2`. The baseline run (the cache miss) uses the same command, so the failing-id set is comparable; the first gate after this piece lands gets its base pass without `-n` (the clerk runs it with `MERGE_GATE_CSIM_XDIST=0` once, or the baseline for that main sha is seeded by hand with the serial command); after that, every base tree carries the guard; `suite_regression.py`'s id parsing is unchanged (xdist prints the same node ids). Determinism: every csim test must give the same result alone and under loadfile; the piece runs the whole suite both ways once and lists any test whose verdict differs as a FINDING (an order-dependent test is a real finding, not a skip). Tests first: a fixture conftest under tmp_path shows the controller builds once and a worker loads `OM_CSIM_LIB` without calling make (the make call is a stub that counts); positive control: today's conftest calls make in every process. Expected gain: the csim stage from about 40 min to under 10 (the measurement report gives the real number before launch).

**HARNESS-gate-tiers (lane H; Tool: Workflow (harness piece: a single piece on shared harness files, lands through the landing clerk; the one question that decided it: a single piece); rev 2, 2026-10-23, audit round 4 folded; owner `scripts/specfactory/merge_gate.sh` (after HARNESS-csim-xdist), `scripts/specfactory/merge_gate_support.py`, `scripts/specfactory/status_check.py` (after HARNESS-tool-line, which edits it first), new `docs/northstar/plans/push/sweeps.md`, `docs/northstar/plans/workflows_2026-10-07/rules.md` section (b) (the landing clerk's procedure) and the "Temporary landing rule" paragraph of `docs/northstar/plans/workflows_2026-10-07/dispatcher.md` [drafter], `tests/specfactory/test_gate_tiers.py`; depends_on per the row: HARNESS-csim-xdist, HARNESS-tool-line, HARNESS-lane-plays-scope, HARNESS-westley-folder, HARNESS-known-xfail, HARNESS-test-edit-row).** [P scripts/specfactory/merge_gate.sh:36-44] [P scripts/specfactory/merge_gate.sh:98-100] [P scripts/specfactory/merge_gate.sh:691-701] [P scripts/specfactory/merge_gate_support.py:1103-1105] [P docs/northstar/spec_factory/symbol_modules.toml:11] [P docs/northstar/spec_factory/symbol_modules.toml:29] [P scripts/specfactory/status_check.py:316-318] [P docs/northstar/plans/workflows_2026-10-07/rules.md:24-25] [P docs/northstar/plans/workflows_2026-10-07/dispatcher.md:68-70] Usage becomes `merge_gate.sh [--revert] [--tier fast|slow|full] <candidate_worktree> <base_ref> <piece_id>[,...]` (mg.sh:42-44; the two flags in either order before the three positionals; default `full` is today's stage list unchanged; `--revert` with `--tier` other than full is usage error 2). The gate prints one new line, `GATE tier <name>` (`<name>` is `fast`, `slow` or `full`), once, before the first stage line, so a test and a clerk can match it exactly; there is no summary line to extend. Fast tier, run by every landing clerk under the lock: mg.sh stages base, make, libm_lint, alg_constants, allowlist_strict, footprint, gd_class_names, symbol_ownership, sig_blocked, sig_check, reach_lint, gd_reach, owner_lint, rule_pins, control_liveness, placeholder_controls, then acceptance and acceptance_godot on the piece's own test files (the row's `acceptance` key plus every file under `tests/` the diff touches), transport, the selected csim files (below) as `regression_csim`, and status; kept besides: godot_import (conditional as today, and required because `acceptance_godot` fails on missing imports in a fresh worktree, `merge_gate.sh:375-382`) and `qa_t1` (a no-op line covered by regression_qa_t1); skipped by the fast tier: regression_lab, regression_qa_t1, specfactory_regression, playthrough, lane_plays, checksum_trace, tactical_trace, undertest_check, size_lint, look. (pre_gate.sh runs before the gate as today and is not a tier.) The fast tier skips stages and reorders none (the step-order test `tests/specfactory/test_gate_a94_steps.py:304` stays green) [drafter]. The csim selection: the csim test files selected by `merge_gate_support.py select-csim <candidate> <base>` (new subcommand): for each changed `csrc/*.c`, every symbol that `merge_gate_support._symbol_module_map` (signatures.toml `module` plus symbol_modules.toml `[symbols]`, `merge_gate_support.py:309-320`) maps to that file (the `[symbols]` rows alone cover 19 of 39 C files and would select nothing for the other 20), then every `tests/csim/test_*.py` whose text contains one of those symbol names (`grep -lF`, no import analysis); a changed `csrc/*.h`, `Makefile` or `tests/csim/conftest.py` selects the whole suite; a diff with no csrc change selects nothing. Interface to the stage: `select-csim` prints one repo-relative path per line, sorted, and nothing else; the stage passes that list as the `test_args` of `merge_gate_support.py regression` (whose parser takes `nargs="+"`, `merge_gate_support.py:1200`), and an empty selection prints `ok regression_csim skipped: no csrc change` and exits 0 without calling `regression`; the baseline for the subset is the same subset run on `base_ref`, and the baseline cache key (`suite_key`, keyed by main sha today, `merge_gate_support.py:565-569`) includes the sorted file list, so a subset run never writes or reads the full-suite cache entry [drafter]. Slow tier, run by a sweep clerk on `main` after every DRAFT-slow-tier-batch (3) landings or DRAFT-slow-tier-hours (2 h), whichever first, outside the lock: `--tier slow` is the full stage list run on a worktree at `origin/main` with `<base_ref>` = the main sha of the last PASS row of `sweeps.md` (the first sweep has no PASS row: its base is `DRAFT-first-sweep-base`, the landing sha of this piece) and `<piece_id>` = the comma list of ids attached since that sha [drafter; candidate, Q7, open for the Architect]; the stages that read the diff `base...HEAD` (footprint, symbol_ownership, rule_pins, control_liveness) run over the union diff of the window, `acceptance` and `acceptance_godot` run for every id in the list, and a reject of any of them on the union diff is a slow-tier REJECT like any other, named by stage in its detail; so `regression_csim` is the full csim regression against the last green sweep, with `acceptance_godot` over the attached ids as the slow tier's gd tests (no new gd-suite stage), playthrough, lane plays and look, each with its per-stage known-red list (`lane_plays_known_red.txt`, `playthrough_known_red.txt`, `gd_tests_known_red.txt`). The sweep clerk appends the run's row to `sweeps.md` (one row per slow-tier run, columns date-time UTC, main sha, PASS or REJECT) and lands it records-only under `records.lock` (DEC-records-lock) [drafter]. A slow-tier REJECT names the landings since the last green sweep (the first-parent shas of `git log --first-parent <last green sha>..origin/main`, printed in the reject detail) [drafter: the sha list]; a bisect clerk reverts the offending landing by `git revert` (`-m 1` for a merge commit), checks the revert with `merge_gate.sh --revert` (on purpose the light revert profile, which stops after owner_lint, `merge_gate.sh:272-277`, not the fast tier) and pushes it under `gate.lock` [drafter: the procedure], and the piece returns to its lane with a FINDING row (the temporary rule's safety net made permanent). `scripts/specfactory/status_check.py` (owned by this row; after HARNESS-tool-line, which edits it first) gains a `sweep` check: it reads `docs/northstar/plans/push/sweeps.md` (new, owned by this row: one row per slow-tier run, columns date-time UTC, main sha, PASS or REJECT) and prints `STATUS sweep WARN` when `git log --first-parent <last green sha>..origin/main` counts more than DRAFT-slow-tier-batch landings or the last green row is older than DRAFT-slow-tier-hours, `STATUS sweep DRIFT` at `DRAFT-slow-tier-drift-mult` (2) times either; the sweep is due at `DRAFT-slow-tier-batch` landings and WARN fires above it, one landing later, and the one unit of "landing" is one first-parent commit as the `git log --first-parent` count reads (the writer parametrises on the constants; whether records pushes and revert commits should count is Q4, open). The no-landing-after-a-red-sweep rule is procedural: the landing clerk reads `sweeps.md` and waits for the bisect, and no stage enforces it (the status stage rejects only DRIFT, `merge_gate.sh:696`). **HARNESS-gate-remote (lane H; Tool: Workflow (harness piece); owner new `scripts/specfactory/gate_remote.sh`, new `docs/northstar/plans/gate_hosts.md`, `tests/specfactory/test_gate_remote.py`; depends_on HARNESS-gate-tiers).** `gate_remote.sh <host> <ref>` runs the slow tier on another machine from a fresh clone: it needs on the host the list the measurement report gives (godot binary and xvfb for the look and plays stages, gcc and make, the Python venv with the gate's packages, no ~ paths: every absolute path in the gate scripts becomes relative to the clone or an environment variable, which this piece fixes in merge_gate.sh and merge_gate_support.py as its one shared-file edit, listed line by line in the row); it rsyncs nothing from the box: the host clones `origin` at `<ref>`, builds, runs `merge_gate.sh --tier slow` with its own baseline cache, and writes the summary and the evidence to a branch `gate-runs/<date>-<ref>` pushed to origin, which the sweep clerk reads. ### Rev 12f (2026-10-12): Westley's answers of the evening (three of nine)

**A17 contact budgets (amends the A17 paragraph).** `[W 2026-10-12: on the march time, "I previously ruled on this, I want to get to the point where I can play the game to change anything. Keep it as it is for now."]` The spawn distance and the march stay as they are; the play budgets follow the measured march (5382 ticks at seed 1 with a 10 percent margin, `DRAFT-a17-contact-budget` 5920 ticks for S-134 and S-136; both are `DRAFT-ground-contact-budget`: space-era measurement reused for ground; Westley's number), so `FINDING-contact-distance` closes as "by design until Westley plays" and the two plays leave `lane_plays_known_red.txt` when A17 lands green. The budgets are not his numbers; a change to the march itself is his after he plays.

**VG-A-startforce (lane A, after A18; three pieces of work: `VG-CONTRACT-force-groups` first (the `mission` frame change, a contract piece with the second-Fable audit, docs first), then `VG-E-startforce` on `csrc/mission.c` placement, then the client piece `VG-A-startforce` on `scenes/tactical_c/tactical_view.gd` control groups; Tool: Workflow for all three (the contract piece touches a pipe frame; the placement is one unit on `csrc/mission.c`, under the three-disjoint-units bar; the client piece ends in the S-103 frame); rev 2, Architect 2026-10-20, after the fresh audit's REJECT (B1 and S1 to S8 folded); rev 2c, 2026-10-22, DEC-C14 home anchor folded in; rev 2b, Architect 2026-10-21, implementation rulings B1, B2, S1, TODO 3, TODO 1 and N3 and the third audit's rulings folded, Westley may veto).** [B R-246-08@c336b6] [B R-105-02@302315: exclusive membership, digit recalls] [P csrc/mission.c:358] [P csrc/mission.c:2464-2530] [P csrc/mission.c:2666-2667] [P csrc/mission.c:2759-2762] [P csrc/mission.c:3112-3116] [P csrc/mission.c:3210] [P csrc/unit.c:2932] [P csrc/pipe/om_pipe.c:819-838] [P tests/csim/test_VG_D_d8.py:28] [P tests/csim/test_VG_E_e7.py:229-240] At mission start the player's force is spawned as three groups. **Split rule** (global, supersedes `DRAFT-startforce-split`'s half-and-quarter wording, whose row is marked superseded by this rev): over the recorded player entries of `force_units` in ascending type then rank order (`mission.c:358`; refused ground walkers and the 4096 cap, `mission.c:280`, mean the recorded entries are the population, so rank is rank among recorded entries), unit j goes to group 1 (the large one) when j mod 4 is 0 or 2, group 2 when j mod 4 is 1, group 3 when j mod 4 is 3 (n = 6 gives 3, 2, 1; an empty group can only happen for n < 4, then groups 2 and 3 may be empty: no node, no pocket, no clamp term). One shared static helper `static uint32_t group_of(uint32_t j)` (j the global rank; the per-type counts are not an input) serves the spawn and the accessor `om_mission_get_force_group(w, i)`, which rebuilds j for entry i by counting recorded player entries before it (a mutant on the helper is the control; test: ids recorded in `force_units[]` ascend with j, a mutant that spawns group by group fails it). The `/* STUB */` block at `mission.c:3112-3116` goes; `docs/northstar/spec_factory/signatures.toml:3568`'s "A stub returning 0 until VG-E-startforce" note is rewritten in the same change (the row owns `signatures.toml` for that line); the stub test `tests/csim/test_VG_E_e7.py:229-240` (asserts the accessor returns 0) is retired under `TD-VG-E-35` (owned by this piece for that test). **Pockets.** Each group has its own cleared pocket on the straight line from the home base B to the objective O, at distance `d_k = min(400 k, 10 k v, 0.15 k |BO|)` from B for k = 1, 2, 3 (large, first small, second small): 400 wu is `DRAFT-startforce-pocket`, `10 k v` is a march of 10 k seconds at v wu per second (`om_unit_profile_speed`, `unit.c:2932`, returns `p->speed` in wu per second; the per-tick step divides it by `OM_TACTICAL_TICK_RATE` at `unit.c:1902`; for a Bastion at 40 wu/s the march term is 400 k and coincides with the pocket draft), v the force's slowest recorded unit's speed (one v for every group, both eras, as R-246-08 says "its slowest unit's speed"), 0.15 is `DRAFT-startforce-clamp`; both drafts are under R-246-08's "at most 30 s march" and are named in Westley's S-103 re-shot checklist (`docs/northstar/plans/decision_pack_2026-10-18/batch.md:63`) for his veto. The sideways offset from the line is `DRAFT-startforce-offset` R = 200 wu: group 1 by R(uy, -ux), group 2 by R(-uy, ux), group 3 by R(uy, -ux), with (ux, uy) the unit vector from B to O (one sqrt for the unit vector and one for |BO|, no other transcendentals). Each pocket is filled ring by ring outward from its nominal centre on the used-cell lattice, so a group reads as a disc, not a row (Westley's S-126 verdict, 2026-10-21: "units are spaced all in a line (unrealistic)"); units are created in j order (type, then rank within type, mission.c:2496-2497, unchanged), each placed at the next free lattice slot of `group_of(j)`'s pocket, so ascending unit id equals j; the three pockets' centres are laid out group 1 at d_1, group 2 at d_2, group 3 at d_3, each with the shared guard, and disc-fill units may sit beyond d_3 by up to the disc radius (the 30 s bound applies to the pocket centre's projection). A used-cell guard shared by the three lists keeps units on distinct cells; pockets may grow and grown pockets may touch (`DRAFT-startforce-maxgroup` is not named: the guard bounds it). n = 0 (start_units 0, or `F_units` 0 with the cap `UINT32_MAX`, `mission.c:2799`): no groups, no pockets, `groups` empty, the spawn loop as today. **Space era** ordering: the field nodes (`mission.c:2759-2762`) are built from the pre-snap B and O, so the pocket centres are defined on the pre-snap line; today there are three nodes (home with `n_player` slots, enemy with `n_enemy`, objective with 0), and the field gets up to six nodes (home, enemy, objective and one per non-empty group): the home node keeps its place with `node_slots` 0 (a default clearing like the objective: the base needs a pocket, the units no longer spawn there), the enemy node is unchanged, and the pocket centres are added as nodes 4 to 6 with `node_slots` the predicted group sizes; a pocket node is present only when its predicted group is non-empty, so the count is 3 + the number of non-empty groups (6 for n >= 4, 3 for n = 0), passed as `n_nodes` in place of `OM_SPACE_FIELD_MIN_NODES` and under `OM_SPACE_FIELD_MAX_NODES` 8; predicted groups come from the whole composed roster F (counts before E7's share) by the same rule; once E7's share is known the real groups are formed and each unit spawns at its group's pocket centre re-snapped to the nearest open cell, so predicted and real groups may differ in size and never in centre. No header change and no internal binding: the pockets are observed through the public mask (`om_space_field_get_mask`: the cell at each pocket centre and its 8 neighbours are clear) and through unit positions (each group's spawn centroid within `DRAFT-startforce-tol` of its pocket centre, computed in the test from the pre-snap B and O); the node-internal "predicted and real centres equal" assertion is dropped and its content is that centroid test (the objective centre is `om_objective_get_center`, IFACE 12). **E7 curve** (absorbed by this piece, Westley C9, 2026-10-21): the starting-force curve is `DRAFT-e7-curve-2`: frac = 0.2 + 0.1 * share up to a half share, then 0.25 + 1.1 * (share - 0.5); the constants are in `csrc/tactical_support.c`, `om_mission_compute_starting_force` (locate by symbol; :469-483 is a hint); the ke2 and e7 pins move under `TD-VG-E-36` in this piece, and the goldens are re-recorded once with the pockets. Tolerance `DRAFT-startforce-tol` = 1.5 x `OM_TERRAIN_CELL_WU` = 150 wu in both eras, against the nominal centre; the centroid tests apply only where consecutive pocket centres are at least 400 wu apart (the unclamped case; all three named fixtures), and where the clamp brings them closer (fast rosters, short BO) only the order of the projections is asserted and the centroids are printed. B and O for the tests: space era the pre-snap constants B = (12000, 15) from `ANCHOR` in `test_VG_D_d8.py:28` (which holds the two faction anchors only) and O = (CANVAS/2, CANVAS/2) as a new constant in `test_VG_E_startforce.py` (the sim moves the real objective to a cell centre, `mission.c:2666-2667`); ground era the reach-snapped px, py after the snap and the midpoint. The ground fixture of the csim and transport tests is the transport scene's exact command stream (`set_seed` 1, 80 regions, features 0 to 7, `mission_start` faction 0 `era_kind` 1, `mission_begin`; `movement_kinds` all walking): under the new curve `DRAFT-e7-curve-2` it gives 55 player units (types 4, 7, 4, 32, 6, 2) in groups 28, 14, 13 by j mod 4 (was 126 units and groups 63, 32, 31 before the curve), archetype 3, region 76 (rev 2's "88 units" was wrong); the client scene's count is re-measured by the shot checker (it serves the S-103 and S-126 shot frames only). `ground_rem_neg2` is no longer "start 5, groups 3, 1, 1": on the new curve it gives 2 units. The n >= 4 small case, the n = 0 case and the n in 1 to 3 case are found by a sweep of start_units on the new curve; the sweep is over the existing csim scene factories (`scene()` of `tests/csim/test_VG_E_e7.py` over archetype x military x seed, `scene()` being defined in `tests/csim/test_VG_E_e1h.py:64`, and the F = 0 scene of `test_held_strongpoint_produces_a_unit` in `test_VG_E_e1h.py` as the first n = 0 candidate); the two picked scenes are pinned with their counts in the `FIXTURES` comment of `tests/csim/test_VG_E_startforce.py`; STOP with a gap if none gives n = 0 or none gives n in 1 to 3; `ground_no_strongpoint` is not an n = 0 fixture (F 602, cap 0). The space fixture is seed 1; a large group near 726 units (F about 2421) is not a fixture. **The frame** (`VG-CONTRACT-force-groups`, lands first, before layerspot and route): `mission.force` gains `groups`, one int per unit in the range 0 to 3 (0 = no group, the accessor's stub sentinel carried until VG-E-startforce lands; 1 to 3 after), appended inside the pipe tally loop (`om_pipe.c:819-838`; a skipped entry contributes nothing, length = sum of types, [] when the force is empty), in the tally's order, the player's units in ascending id order; `docs/architecture.md:304` gains this clause verbatim: "`groups` is one int per player unit in the tally's order (ascending unit id): 0 no group, else 1 to 3, the starting control group (R-246-08, om_mission_get_force_group); groups[i] belongs to the i-th lowest id among the player's units (faction equal to the player's, 0 in the pipe) in the first full tactical frame after mission_begin; length equals the sum of types, [] when the force is empty. Derived at read time; nothing saved or hashed, no ABI change. After E7 the tally is the starting force, start_units; F and the reinforcement allowance are not in the frame." (that closes FU-E7-force-frame, whose owner is `VG-CONTRACT-force-groups`, the ledger row wins); the pins under `TD-VG-E-31`: `tests/transport/test_pipe_mission_frame_briefing_fields.py:77` (`set(force) == {"types", "tiers"}` gains `"groups"`, with length and range 0..3 asserted), the control at :94-97 adds `"groups": []`, the examples at :102-103 carry it, the mutants at :121-122 gain a wrong-length and a value-4 case; the tightening to 1..3 is VG-E-startforce's, in a new transport test `tests/transport/test_pipe_force_groups_values.py` (a csim test cannot read a frame); the accessor rides E7's ABI 8 (no new ABI). **Position pins** under `TD-VG-E-35`: `tests/transport/test_pipe_units_move.py:74-81` (Raider 21 and Bastion 294 at fixed positions, moved once already by TD-VG-E-34) move to the new pockets; `tests/transport/test_pipe_vg_identity.py::test_new_pipe_views_equal_mains_without_the_new_keys` goes red again on ground positions against the E7 main and its known-red line returns for this piece's landing under `TD-VG-E-35` (the landing clerk deletes it once main carries the placement); `d8_a`'s 600 wu bound is not widened: its scope narrows to the nearest pocket (k = 1 lies within 400 wu of B, inside the bound), `d8_e` is narrowed the same way, `d8_b`'s bound becomes anchor distance minus 2 x CLEAR_RADIUS_WU minus d_3 (the player's farthest pocket), under `TD-VG-E-35`, `d8_d` is re-checked and listed; the goldens that move are the ids `TD-VG-E-26` lists (`test_E13_A99_tactical_equivalence.py`, `test_PP_C_g3_hash_invariant.py`, `parity/baseline/test_baseline_hashes.py`, `test_PP_target_policy_per_side.py`, `test_PP_portability.py`, and `tests/csim/test_E01_T3_mission_ground.py` with its 3 ids: six files), re-recorded once by this piece; the builder runs the full csim and transport suites on the built tree and lists every position-dependent red in its report, each under one of the two rows or stopped as a finding. R-246-07 (E-17 :397, "the army spawns at its home base") is rewritten by this piece to "the player's army spawns in three pockets on the line from its home base toward the objective, the first beside the base, none farther than R-246-08's march, and the home base anchor sits 1200 wu in from its map edge on the centre line (`DRAFT-home-anchor-inboard`)" (rewrite, never append). The cap hash: `FU-E7-reinforce-cap-hash`'s isolation test (the cap reaches the mission hash and the save bytes, positive control on `reinforce_spawned`) is in this piece's test list; the test asserts `checksum_full` and the save bytes differ, through the mission section hash `om_mis_net_hash` (`mission.c:3201`, the cap at :3210); `checksum_full.c` is not edited. **Client** (`VG-A-startforce`): binds control groups 1, 2 and 3 from `groups` in the first full tactical frame after mission_begin, before it is drawn (a mission resumed by load gets no mission frame, `om_pipe.c:1363`, so after a load nothing is bound and the player's saved control groups stand), as if the player had pressed Ctrl plus the digit (R-105-02 exclusive membership holds), joining `groups[i]` to the i-th player unit by ascending id; it also rewrites the old R-246-07 wording in the docstrings `tests/test_tactical_camera.gd:37,589` and `tests/qa/desktop/plays/A/test_S_103_start_view.py:7` (FU-startforce-gd-docstrings); the row adds both files to its `owns` in the launch records commit. `qa_state` gains `control_groups`: `{"1": [ids], "2": [ids], "3": [ids]}`, ascending ids, empty lists when unbound; on the ground fixture the sizes are the split counts of the fixture sentence. Client tests: `tests/test_vg_a_startforce.gd` (inventory assertion on `control_groups`, then press 1 and assert the selection equals group 1) and the play `tests/qa/desktop/plays/A/test_S_105_start_groups.py` (`qa_state` plus the S-105 shot). **Tests first** (sim, `tests/csim/test_VG_E_startforce.py`): for each group the centroid's projection on the BO line is within `DRAFT-startforce-tol` of `d_k` and its perpendicular distance is within the tolerance of R on the stated side, in order; each unit's pocket is reachable (R-246-07 carve); the split counts and the n < 4 and n = 0 cases (one test each); no group's units lie on one line (the least-squares line residual of a group of 15 or more units is above one cell; smaller groups are printed); the ground-era fixture: the split, the order of the projections, no two groups share a position; the space-era pockets observed through the public mask and the unit centroids (see Space era ordering); the accessor's rebuilt j equals i over the player's recorded entries, so its grouping equals the spawn's, on the fixture; determinism (same seed, same groups); the cap hash test; contract: `groups` length equals the tally and its values are 0 to 3 at the contract piece and 1 to 3 after VG-E-startforce (`tests/transport/test_pipe_force_groups_values.py`); contract control: the :121-122 mutants gain a wrong-length and a value-4 frame, both rejected by `_check_frame`; client: after `mission_begin` the three groups are bound and pressing 1 selects exactly the large group; ids: units are created in today's order (type, then count; `mission.c:2510-2524`), so ids ascend with j and the tally's ascending-id order is j order, only each unit's position comes from its group's pocket, and a test asserts `om_mission_get_force_unit` ids ascend with j; positive controls: a one-pocket spawn fails the spacing test, an unbound client fails the recall test. Shot: the S-103 opening frame (A18 frames every own unit, so all three groups are in view) queued for Westley with the pocket distances in the story_reviews queue row. **Home anchor inboard** (DEC-C14-2026-10-22, folded into this piece so the position goldens move once; rev 2c, Architect 2026-10-22). **VG-A-sightdisc (lane A fix piece; closes `FINDING-s126-vision-circle-orientation`, `FINDING-sightdisc-mask-behind-camera` and `FINDING-sightdisc-rings-fail-triangulation`; Tool: Workflow (ends in a frame); owner `scenes/tactical_c/fog_layer.gd`, the fog shader it loads, `scenes/tactical_c/tactical_view.gd` (the `sight_discs` clause), `tests/test_vg_a_fog_layer.gd` (retired lines `:176-178`), `tests/test_vg_c_land_one_projection.gd` (under `TD-VG-C-land-one-projection-3`: the :228 empty assertion was a placeholder), new `tests/test_vg_a_sightdisc.gd` (its fourth clip test replaced under `TD-VG-A-sightdisc-2`); depends_on per the row (VG-A, HARNESS-provenance-scope); shot: the S-005 sight frame re-shot at the C-S005 camera (height 300, run-20261021T221300Z-zoomed, tick 9774), queued for Westley as the story's verdict shot (rejected 2026-10-22); it is taken after this piece and VG-C-ground-one-colour both land, whichever lands last queues it).** [W 2026-10-21: "vision 'circle' is rotated to be parrallel with the screen, not the units and the current orientation of the landscape they're on. (In starcraft 2, the vision circle isn't a perfect circle from the users view, it's a perfect circle if you look straight down, which this screenshot isn't)"; W 2026-10-22: "from the foundation up the vision circle should be derived from the unit, and drawn as a circle that exists separately from the user perspective"] [B R-089-03@c09d1f] [P scenes/tactical_c/fog_layer.gd:60-106] Today `fog_layer.gd` keeps one screen circle per own unit (centre, radius averaged from two projected offsets) and the shader punches round holes, so the disc is parallel to the screen. The set_terrain call and the qa_state key are VG-C-land-one-projection's; this piece reads them. Interface: `FogLayer.set_terrain(terrain: Dictionary)` (the frame's terrain dict: `bands`, `layer_floors_wu`, cell size `CELL_WU` 100 from `camera_rig_3d.gd:20`), called from `tactical_view.gd` where `_terrain` is set; the height-aware projection is `Ground3DHost.to_screen`, which already projects at the terrain height (`ground3d.gd:126-127`). Stated unchanged: the `fog_layer` qa key stays a count; `screen_points()` stays `Array[Vector2]` (`tests/support/vg_c_case.gd:63-79`). Retired test: `tests/test_vg_a_fog_layer.gd:176-178` (`radius`, `center`) under `TD-VG-A-sightdisc` (test-defect, assumption-changed: discs are polygons); the row covers every `["center"]` and `["radius"]` read in that file [P tests/test_vg_a_fog_layer.gd:176-178] [P tests/test_vg_a_fog_layer.gd:181] [P tests/test_vg_a_fog_layer.gd:191]: A4's radius assertion becomes "the farthest point from the polygon's centroid is sight x world_scale within 0.5 px", and the centre assertions and the R-089-04 check use the centroid. Space path: with no ground host the projector is `(p - camera) * world_scale + half`, no terrain, so the 48 points form the old circle and the 2D look is unchanged. New: each own unit's sight disc is the ground region within `sight` wu of the unit, projected through the camera: `_discs[i]` becomes `{points: PackedVector2Array}` of `DRAFT-sightdisc-segments` 48 screen points, point k = projector(unit ground position + sight × (cos θk, sin θk), at the terrain height of that ground point when the terrain layer has one, else the unit's height); the fog mask is a polygon mask: the one-row float texture goes, the shader samples a screen-sized mask texture that `fog_layer.gd` draws from the polygons into a `SubViewport` each frame (holes where polygons are: the mask hole is the 2D polygon of the disc's ground points clipped in world space against the camera near plane, then projected, the plane offset by DRAFT-sightdisc-clip-eps 0.5 wu; Architect draft, Westley vetoes); `sight_discs()` returns the polygons and `screen_points()` their centroids (callers unchanged); `qa_state()["sight_discs"]`: per own unit id (string) the screen bbox [x, y, w, h] of the disc polygon's points in front of the camera, clamped to the viewport, the bbox being that of the clipped polygon; a disc with no such point has no entry. The fill skips points behind the camera (`Camera3D.is_position_behind` on the world point through `Ground3DHost`), clamps the box to the viewport and omits the entry when none survive. The builder reports before commit whether the drawn mask is a 3D ground polygon or drawn from the same 2D projected points (if 2D: FINDING on sightdisc, landing stops for a fix brief). `qa_state()["sight_discs_failed"]` (int, beside `sight_discs`): the counter of mask rings whose `Geometry2D.triangulate_polygon` is empty, reset in `set_frame`; rings of fewer than 3 points after clipping count as skipped, not failed. Test `test_mask_rings_triangulate`: land fixture at camera heights 300 then 3000, `sight_discs_failed` == 0 and every `sight_discs` entry inside the viewport. At pitch 90 (the 2D top-down view) the polygon is a circle, so the 2D look is unchanged. Tests first (`tests/test_vg_a_sightdisc.gd`): at the 3D rig's pitch 50 the bbox height over width is sin(50°) = 0.766 within `DRAFT-sightdisc-ratio-tol` 0.05; at pitch 90 it is 1.0 within 0.02 (the same draft at pitch 90); close-zoom camera height `DRAFT-sightdisc-close-height` 300; a unit on a slope has its disc points at the terrain heights (a planted ramp: the far points sit higher on screen than a flat disc would put them); positive control: on main the ratio at pitch 50 reads 1.0, so the first test is red for the stated reason. Mask control: a test samples the SubViewport texture at a point inside and a point outside the polygon: inside is lit, outside is dimmed; on main the polygon path does not exist, red. Positioning-decides rule unchanged: the disc only draws what R-089-03 already computes. The screen-circle path is deleted in the same change, not left beside the new one (Westley 2026-10-22: no old code lying around): the centre-and-radius `_discs` form, the one-row float texture and the hole-punch shader branch go; a test asserts `fog_layer.gd` holds no radius disc field and the shader declares no float-texture uniform (positive control: main fails it). The ground-plane projection also fixes the close-zoom vanishing (FINDING-fog-dim-close-zoom); a test at camera height 300. Amendment 2026-10-22 (Westley: "when you're walking up a ramp in SC2 you can't see anything at the top of the ramp until you cross a threshold"; Architect, implementation): the disc is clipped to ground the unit may see. Along each of the 48 rays the piece walks the terrain cells from the unit outward in `OM_TERRAIN_CELL_WU` steps and stops at the first cell whose band (from the frame's terrain `bands`, a slope cell counted by its own band value in `bands`, the one-cell error at a midline accepted, DRAFT-sightdisc-slope-cell; a band-0 cell counts as the unit's band) is higher than the unit's band; the ray's point is the last allowed cell's far edge or the full sight radius, whichever comes first. Looking down is unclipped (R-005-09: high ground sees down). Clip semantics are cosmetic: the disc shows the ground the unit can see by band, and `sight` is the frame's `sight` field. Ramp fixture: 8 by 8 cells, bands 1 on columns 0 to 3 and 2 on columns 4 to 7, heights 0 and 300 wu. Tests: a unit on the lower band below a planted ramp lights no point past the ramp's midline; the same unit moved above the midline lights the ramp and the lower ground to its full radius; positive control: on main both discs are full circles. The clip only draws what `csrc/fog.c:730` already decides (an upward look in a ground era returns 0.0), so no unit becomes visible or hidden by it.

**VG-E1M (lane E part b, S-235, the ticker beats; the E1m paragraph the "S-235 ticker" amendment refers to; owns `scenes/economy/ticker_beats.gd` (new, class `TickerBeats`), `scenes/tactical_c/hud/events_feed.gd` (the three-line skip only, ruling g2 (b), Architect 2026-10-14; VG-A is the attached glob owner) and `tests/test_vg_e_e1m.gd`; depends_on per the row (VG-E1, VG-A18); Tool: Workflow (one client piece that ends in the S-235 frame; built 2026-10-19 on `origin/push/vg-e1m` d0988ac5 with tests first by a different writer on `origin/push/vg-e1m-tests` 5b3aa6bc, so only the landing remains: it lands with VG-E1N and VG-E1W from `origin/push/vg-e1screens-pins` 628ca180 under DEC-combined-landings-2026-10-23, or alone first since its files are disjoint from E1N's; Tool question 2: needs Godot, question 3: one unit; `scenes/economy/ticker_beats.gd` and `tests/test_vg_e_e1m.gd` also fall under the attached glob VG-E1 and `events_feed.gd` under VG-A, the existing pattern; the queued VG-E-a8feed also owns `events_feed.gd` and waits on this piece).** `[W 2026-10-12: Q-235-01 (c), "the ticker carries the four beats plus a strongpoint taken or lost; forward bases are not named"]` [B R-235-03@14ac1a] [B R-104-01@1aaaa3] [B R-104-02@03d7aa] [B R-235-01@4f35af] [B R-235-02@138056] [B R-235-04@a81fce] (R-235-03, Westley 2026-10-12, extends R-104-01's four-beat list for the ticker and governs the beat count: five beats, the four economy beats plus a strongpoint taken or lost; R-104-02 is the banner time; the test header pins all six rules) [P scenes/tactical_c/hud/events_feed.gd:5] [P scenes/tactical_c/hud/events_feed.gd:94-96] Today A8's feed says on its header line that "TickerBeats (lane E1m) feeds more beats through add_beat()" and `_connect_ticker` finds a node named `TickerBeats` by `get_tree().root.find_child("TickerBeats", true, false)` and connects its signal `beat` to `add_beat(kind: String, text: String)`; no such node exists on main. Interface (frozen, the INTERFACE block of `vg-wave13-laneEscreens.js`): `TickerBeats extends Node`, script only, `_ready` sets `name = "TickerBeats"`; `signal beat(kind: String, text: String)`; `const KINDS := ["base_occupied", "fallback", "expansion", "weather", "strongpoint_taken", "strongpoint_lost"]` (the five beats of R-235-03, the strongpoint beat carried as two kind strings, taken and lost); `const TEXTS` kind to text, the six texts "Base occupied", "Fell back to an expansion", "Expansion founded", "Weather arriving", "Strongpoint taken", "Strongpoint lost", wording `DRAFT-e1m-beat-text` (ledger row on the branch, rides the landing; Westley may reword after the shot; the S-235 play's detector reads the words "Strongpoint taken" and "Strongpoint lost" from the Events label, so a reword edits `tests/qa/desktop/plays/E/S-235.py` `beat_kind` in the same change); `func emit_beat(kind: String) -> bool` emits `beat(kind, TEXTS[kind])` and returns true for a kind in KINDS, emits nothing and returns false for any other string (a shipment, R-235-04, or a forward base is never a beat); `func feed(frame: Dictionary, local_faction: int) -> void` takes the reconstructed tactical view that `tactical_view.gd` already hands to `Events.feed` [P scenes/tactical_c/tactical_view.gd:530] (the E1w wiring adds the sibling call `_node("TickerBeats").feed(frame, _get_local_faction())`); `func beats_emitted() -> int` and `func last_beat() -> Dictionary` (`{kind, text}` or `{}`) for tests and qa. Frame source, real (ruling g2 (b)): a strongpoint is a structure whose id is its structure slot [P csrc/strongpoint.c:47] and its owner mirrors `om_structure_get_owner` [P csrc/strongpoint.c:16-21]; the pipe writes `strongpoints` on full frames only [P csrc/pipe/pipe_tactical.c:231-234] and the bridge keeps that list from the last full frame [P autoload/c_bridge.gd:509] [P autoload/c_bridge.gd:547], so its owner field goes stale between full frames, while the live owner is in the `structures` rows [P csrc/pipe/pipe_tactical.c:105-129] that the bridge folds every delta into [P autoload/c_bridge.gd:523-527] [P autoload/c_bridge.gd:545]; `feed` therefore joins the ids of `frame["strongpoints"]` to the owners of `frame["structures"]`, remembers the owner per id, emits nothing on the first frame it sees (A8's first-sight rule), emits one `strongpoint_taken` when a remembered owner that was not `local_faction` becomes it and one `strongpoint_lost` for the reverse, and counts a strongpoint that is own and then absent from the structures rows (destroyed: the pipe skips it and sends `structures_removed`) as lost once; a strongpoint that is not own and is absent from the rows keeps its remembered owner and emits nothing (the pipe drops a structure that is neither the player's nor in sight when `g_fog_filter` is set, so absence is not destruction for a non-own id [P csrc/pipe/pipe_tactical.c:113]); a strongpoint passing between two other factions and an ordinary structure flipping are no beat. Stub (ruling g3 (a)): `base_occupied`, `fallback`, `expansion` and `weather` have no sim source (the tactical frame's economy key is `{doctrine, resources:{}}` [P csrc/pipe/pipe_tactical.c:296]; the wave log logs kinds 3 and 4 only and no pipe frame carries `om_wave_get_since`; `git grep -i weather csrc` is empty) and are reachable through `emit_beat` and the A8 listening path only; the file header says STUB and names the four ledger rows on main `FU-E1m-beat-base-occupied`, `FU-E1m-beat-fall-back`, `FU-E1m-beat-expansion-founded`, `FU-E1m-beat-weather-arrives` (ledger :1158-1161; the built header cites them under four wrong ids and says the clerk adds them: the clerk of the combined landing under this paragraph corrects that one comment line, and may edit nothing else in the file, before the gate; no ledger row is added for it). The events_feed.gd change is exactly three lines plus one field [P scenes/tactical_c/hud/events_feed.gd:36-45]: a `_strongpoints` set filled from `frame.get("strongpoints", [])` in `feed` and a `continue` in the `structures_changed` loop for an id in that set after the owner is remembered, so one ticker line per strongpoint event; `_strongpoints` is never cleared, the same as the file's existing `_owners` and `_progress`; strongpoint ids are structure slots fixed at init, so within one mission the set is right, and the stale-across-missions behaviour is accepted as the existing one; nothing else in the file moves (`add_beat` :60, `MAX_LINES` 6 at :10 and `BANNER_S` 4.0 at :7 are read, not changed). Known inert path: `c_bridge.gd` erases `structures_changed` before the view reaches `Events.feed` [P autoload/c_bridge.gd:542-544] (FINDING-a8-captured-never-shows), so in play A8 beats nothing and the skip is proven in isolation only; VG-E-a8feed (depends_on VG-E1M) restores A8's lines and keeps this skip (its paragraph, not yet on main, says so at both sites). Player-facing inventory: the ticker is `Hud/Events` and its first `Label` holds the lines (`MAX_LINES` 6, newest last) with the banner on the second label for `BANNER_S` 4 s; `qa_state()["hud"]["events"]` is the line count [P scenes/tactical_c/tactical_view.gd:638]; no new qa key (the gap 9 null action recorded in the lane E screens script waives one; the kind is read from the label text by the play); the inventory is therefore `hud.events` (the count) plus the `Events.lines()` text asserted in [A8], [FEED] and [SKIP]; the S-235 play (`tests/qa/desktop/plays/E/S-235.py`, VG-E1W's) asserts the ticker empty at tick 0, marches to contact and takes the shot at the first line whose text is "Strongpoint taken" or "Strongpoint lost" with no "Structure ..." line for the same event, within `DRAFT-s235-budget` 5920 ticks (VG-E1W's ledger row); no beat by the cap is RED and a FINDING (Architect round 3), never a label. Tests first (`tests/test_vg_e_e1m.gd`, on the test branch, headless, no display waiver; extends `tests/support/vg_c_case.gd`): [CONTROL] green on the built tree, a stub node named `TickerBeats` with `signal beat(kind, text)` beside a real `events_feed.gd` puts its text into `Events.lines()` and raises the banner (the positive control for every group: the listening path A8 already ships); [LOAD] `class_name TickerBeats`, the signal, KINDS exactly the six strings, TEXTS six non-empty strings none mentioning a shipment or a forward base, `_ready` name and findability; [EMIT] `emit_beat` per kind and false for `shipment` and `forward_base`; [A8] a real TickerBeats beat reaches `Events.lines()` through `_connect_ticker`; [FEED] the first-frame rule, taken, no second beat on the same frame, lost on the flip back, the ordinary-structure and third-party controls, `local_faction` 1, own strongpoint absent from the rows is one lost once, a non-own strongpoint absent from the rows emits nothing and keeps its owner (the fog control); [SKIP] an ordinary structure still gives exactly one "Structure captured"/"Structure lost" line (green on the built tree, the positive control that A8 still beats ordinary structures) and a strongpoint id gives no Structure line and exactly one strongpoint line (red on main: A8 beats it), owners still remembered for the next ordinary flip, PIN a8-feed: a delta that itself carries `strongpoints` is skipped; [NOSOURCE] an economy key with resources, doctrine and a weather key never emits the four stub kinds while the same path emits the one real beat. Red on the built tree for the stated reason (`scenes/economy/ticker_beats.gd` missing, A8 beats a strongpoint); the failing assertion line is pasted by the landing's pre-gate record, not here; `tests/test_vg_a_events_feed.gd` stays unedited and green (no existing test encodes a superseded ruling: that file never feeds a `strongpoints` key). Retired text: the lane E1 piece-list sentence "no edit to events_feed.gd" was struck by ruling g2 (b) (2026-10-14) and already reads as amended on main. **VG-E1W (lane E, S-235, the wiring piece of the economy screens; owns `scenes/tactical_c/tactical_view.gd`, `tests/test_vg_e_e1w.gd`, `tests/qa/desktop/plays/E/S-235.py`; depends_on VG-E1, VG-A18, VG-D-VIEW, VG-E1M, VG-E1N, VG-C-land-marks, VG-C-land-one-projection per the row; Tool: Workflow (ends in a frame: the S-235 shot; a wiring piece on the shared file; one landing with E1m and E1n), the existing `vg-wave13-laneEscreens.js` relaunched with its args (tests and builds skipped to the branches below), not a fresh build; Architect's rulings g2, g3, g5, g6, g8, g10 of 2026-10-14 stand, restated where this paragraph uses them, with their full text in `BUILTIN_RULINGS` of `vg-wave13-laneEscreens.js`; Q-104-02 answered 2026-10-23: "B for beta" (the ticker carries six beats, R-104-01 as rewritten).)** `[W 2026-10-12: Q-235-01 (c)]` `[B R-235-01@4f35af]` `[B R-235-02@138056]` `[B R-235-03@14ac1a]` `[B R-235-04@a81fce]` `[B R-104-01@1aaaa3]` (Q-104-02 answered 2026-10-23: "B for beta"; R-104-01 is rewritten to the six beats) [P scenes/tactical_c/tactical_view.gd:42] [P scenes/tactical_c/tactical_view.gd:166-170] [P scenes/tactical_c/tactical_view.gd:487-534] [P scenes/tactical_c/tactical_view.gd:546-594] [P scenes/tactical_c/tactical_view.gd:623-657] [P scenes/tactical_c/tactical_view.gd:1378-1384] [P scenes/tactical_c/hud/events_feed.gd:94-96] [P autoload/c_bridge.gd:538-549] E1m (`scenes/economy/ticker_beats.gd`, on origin/push/vg-e1m) and E1n (`scenes/economy/map_markers.gd`, on origin/push/vg-e1n-fix) exist as nodes nobody instantiates; the A8 ticker already listens for a node named `TickerBeats` (`events_feed.gd:94-96`); `tactical_view.gd` on main builds its shell from the `PIECES` table (:42) in `_build_shell` (:169), feeds `Events` in `load_frame` (:530) and redraws the frame-bound pieces in `_redraw` (:1378). This piece puts the two nodes in the tree and on the frame path, nothing else. Interface, fixed in full (E1m and E1n are its producers, the test writer and the play its consumers): `TickerBeats` extends Node, `signal beat(kind: String, text: String)`, `KINDS = ["base_occupied", "fallback", "expansion", "weather", "strongpoint_taken", "strongpoint_lost"]`, `TEXTS` per kind (`DRAFT-e1m-beat-text`), `emit_beat(kind: String) -> bool`, `feed(frame: Dictionary, local_faction: int) -> void` (a strongpoint id whose `structures` owner becomes the local faction emits `strongpoint_taken`, one that stops being it emits `strongpoint_lost`, first frame primes without emitting), `beats_emitted() -> int`, `last_beat() -> Dictionary`; `MapMarkers` extends Node2D, `set_frame(frame: Dictionary, local_faction: int, world_scale: float, camera: Vector2) -> void`, `markers() -> Array` of `{kind: String ("strongpoint" | "home_base"), x: float, y: float, owner: int, state: int}`, strongpoints ascending id then the one home base (the kind-7 map node nearest the first own-unit centroid, remembered once per local faction, FINDING-e1n-home-base-follows-army). The six edits to `tactical_view.gd` (the whole diff on origin/push/vg-e1w 016943a5, re-applied on current main): (1) `PIECES` gains `"MapMarkers": "res://scenes/economy/map_markers.gd"` and `"TickerBeats": "res://scenes/economy/ticker_beats.gd"`; (2) `_build_shell` adds `MapMarkers` between `StructuresLayer` and `ObjectiveMarkers` in the shell list (marks under the objective marks, over the structures), and `TickerBeats` as a child of the view, not of `Hud`, with the comment that `events_feed.gd` finds it by name (g5: TickerBeats is a child of the view); (3) `load_frame` calls `_node("TickerBeats").feed(frame, _get_local_faction())` on the line after the `Events` feed, so the strongpoint beat and the A8 beats come from the same frame; (4) `qa_state()` gains `"map_markers": _node("MapMarkers").markers()` (the full rows); (5) `_inventory()` gains `"map_markers": <that size>`; (6) the `_redraw` frame-bound list becomes `["StructuresLayer", "MapMarkers", "Shots", "Deaths", "Abilities", "Ambient"]`, each getting `set_frame(_frame, lf, _world_scale, _camera)`. Stated unchanged: `events_feed.gd` (E1m's file), `hud.events` stays the line count, `strongpoints` is not a qa key of this piece (E1p/E1l, s218 known red), no new node under `Hud`, the land view's marks projection is VG-C-land-one-projection's and E1w does not route `MapMarkers` through it (an E1n follow-up if the 2D marks sit wrong in the 3D ground view: question 2). Ordering (settled by the Architect 2026-10-23): E1W lands after VG-C-land-marks, so its edits to the shared file are re-applied on top of land-marks' in the same ranges; the row's older "lands first" comment is deleted; the one known conflict is the `qa_state` dictionary, where `map_markers` goes after `sight_discs`. Tests first by a different writer (on origin/push/vg-e1w-tests 4c8d7cde, red on the built tree before 016943a5 for the stated reason: no node named `MapMarkers`, `qa_state` has no `map_markers` key; 173 checks, 3 red on the first E1n build caught the home-base drift): `tests/test_vg_e_e1w.gd` (NEEDS A DISPLAY, FU-E1n-pregate-display: pre-gate and clerk run it under `xvfb-run -a`) with groups [CONTROL] (a stub `TickerBeats` beside a real `events_feed.gd`: its emit lands in `Events.lines()`, the positive control of every later group), [NODES] (the two nodes by name from the root), [KEYS] (a fixture frame with three strongpoints at owners 1, -1, 0 and one kind-7 node gives four markers of the stated shape, kinds `[strongpoint x3, home_base]`, a non-strongpoint structure unmarked), [BEAT] (enemy to local on strongpoint 5 adds exactly one `strongpoint_taken` line and no `Structure captured` (g2: E1m's three-line skip in `events_feed.gd` drops the structure line for a strongpoint id; this assert needs E1m's `events_feed.gd` edit in the same landing, and is trivially green on main until VG-E-a8feed lands); local to enemy one `strongpoint_lost`; neither-side changes and non-strongpoint structures add nothing; an own strongpoint in `structures_removed` adds one `strongpoint_lost`), [HOME] (the army marched onto the enemy's kind-7 node keeps the home mark at the own spawn), [ORDER] (each of the six kinds through `emit_beat` is exactly one line with its `TEXTS` entry, none says "shipment" or "forward"; an unknown kind returns false and adds no line; the banner never names a shipment, R-235-04), [REACH] (frames loaded only through `load_frame` leave the home mark present: wired, not just tested). The play `tests/qa/desktop/plays/E/S-235.py` (ground era `greek_placeholder`, seed 1, 1920x1080, vulkan, `set_fog_filter` off as S-134): detector controls without the game; then `map_markers` non-empty at tick 0 with a `strongpoint` and a `home_base`, every kind in `{strongpoint, home_base}`, `inventory.map_markers` equal to its length, the ticker empty; select the army by drag, attack-move to the nearest enemy, `step_ticks` in blocks of 25 up to `DRAFT-s235-budget` 5920 ticks (5382 measured march plus 10 percent, ledger row DRAFT-s235-budget, Westley's number; question 1) and stop at the first ticker line that names a strongpoint; no line by the cap is RED and a finding, never a skip or a labelled shot; then the mark that changed hands (else the first strongpoint mark) is brought on screen (`project`, else `_edge_scroll_to`) and `moment(c, "S-235", "ticker_and_markers")` is the shot. Inventory asserted on the shot: `map_markers` still holds a `strongpoint` and the `home_base`, ints for `owner` and `state`; `hud.events` at least 1; the strongpoint lines are exactly one and of kind `strongpoint_taken` or `strongpoint_lost`; no `Structure` line came with it (g2); no line outside the R-104-01 beats and the strongpoint beats; none mentions a shipment. Stubs, said in the report and the queue row: the four economy beats (FU-E1m-beat-base-occupied, -fall-back, -expansion-founded, -weather-arrives) the marks for economic locations, neutral settlements, expansions, forward works and weather (g6: only `strongpoint` and `home_base` are drawn, every other R-235-01 kind a stub; R-235-02 has no sim weather), and the map view (S-161) marks, which R-235-01 also names and which no piece builds yet. Shot and verdict: one S-235 shot set, the landing clerk copies it into `docs/northstar/story_reviews/E.md` with a queue row at landing and puts the `story_reviews/westley/` copy in at landing with its README row (g10: one shot per lane); the marker look (diamond 36 px, cross for rubble, filled square for the home base, StructuresLayer colours: `DRAFT-e1n-marker`) and the beat wording (`DRAFT-e1m-beat-text`) are judged by eye on that shot, so Westley's word on it is the prototype gate for any marker or wording change, and no look is guessed here. Retired tests: none (a grep of `tests/` for exact-key and child-order assertions on `qa_state()["inventory"]` and the shell children, `grep -rn "get_child(\|get_children()\|inventory.*size()" tests/test_vg_a*.gd tests/test_qa_daemon_inventory.gd`, found one unrelated hit in `test_vg_a_loading_overlay.gd:161`); `tests/test_vg_a_events_feed.gd` stays unedited and green (g2). Numbers: `DRAFT-s235-budget` 5920 ticks and `BLOCK_TICKS` 25 (the TD-VG-A-1 chunk) in the play; `DRAFT-e1n-marker` 36 px and 3 px and `DRAFT-e1m-beat-text` in E1n's and E1m's files; this piece adds no number of its own.

**VG-E-a8feed (lane E fix piece, wave 9; closes `FINDING-a8-captured-never-shows`; owns `scenes/tactical_c/hud/events_feed.gd` (after VG-E1M lands; VG-E1M's explicit own stands until then and VG-A is the attached glob owner, the E1M pattern), `tests/test_vg_e_a8feed.gd` and `tests/qa/desktop/plays/E/S-127.py` (a new file; both under the attached VG-E1 globs, the sanctioned pattern); depends_on VG-E1M (its three-line skip is the base, built on `origin/push/vg-e1m` d0988ac5) and VG-E1N (ordering only, DEC-combined-landings-2026-10-23); Tool: Workflow (ends in a frame: one client piece, one test file, one play and a shot; a single unit, so no cascade); Architect 2026-10-23 (drafted), implementation, Westley may veto.** [B R-127-04@db7974] [B R-104-01@1aaaa3] [B R-104-02@03d7aa] [B R-235-03@14ac1a] [P autoload/c_bridge.gd:542-544] [P autoload/c_bridge.gd:549] [P scenes/tactical_c/hud/events_feed.gd:31-45] [P scenes/tactical_c/tactical_view.gd:530] [P scenes/tactical_c/tactical_view.gd:638] [P csrc/pipe/pipe_tactical.c:111-120] Today `tactical_view.gd:530` hands `Events.feed` the reconstructed view, which `c_bridge.gd:542-543` has stripped of `structures_changed` and `:549` has marked `full = true` with the whole `structures` list, so `events_feed.gd:33-35` overwrites `_owners` from that list and the only beat path, `:36-45`, never runs in play: zero captured or lost lines across three flips (the finding's scout probe), and the S-235 one-line-per-event assertion holds trivially from `TickerBeats` alone. Design (the row's "builder picks the smaller", decided here as the feed diff): `c_bridge.gd` is not touched; `feed(frame: Dictionary, local_faction: int) -> void` (signature unchanged, callers unchanged) diffs owners from the `structures` rows the same way E1M's `TickerBeats.feed` does for strongpoints. New `structures` branch, guarded by `frame.has("structures")` (raw deltas reach `feed` with no `structures` key, `tests/test_vg_a_events_feed.gd:185-187`, and a frame without the key touches `_owners` not at all): for each row `{id, owner}` of `frame["structures"]`, with `now = int(owner)`: an id not in `_owners` is remembered silently (A8's first-sight rule, `test_vg_a_events_feed.gd` CONTROL "a first frame is not a beat"); a remembered id whose owner changed and whose id is not in `_strongpoints` (E1M's skip, kept at this site with a `# See also: scenes/economy/ticker_beats.gd` line) adds one `add_beat("captured", "Structure captured")` when `was != local_faction and now == local_faction`, one `add_beat("lost", "Structure lost")` when `was == local_faction and now != local_faction`, nothing for a third-party flip; `_owners[id] = now` in every case. A structure that leaves the rows (destroyed, or an own structure dropped by the fog filter) emits nothing and nothing reads `structures_removed`: R-127-04 names an owner change only, and the absence beat is the open question Q-127-05 (candidates in the register: (a) no beat, the structure line simply stops; (b) a "lost" beat when an own structure flips), so the first draft's absence rule, its [ABSENT] tests and `DRAFT-a8feed-absent-is-lost` are removed; the fog gap (an own structure that flips to the enemy out of sight leaves the rows and so never beats, the pipe's non-own out-of-sight skip at pipe_tactical.c:113-116) is part of Q-127-05, and the builder adds nothing for it. The `structures_changed` branch (`:36-45` on main, `:40-50` on the E1M branch) stays as the raw-delta path that `tests/test_vg_a_events_feed.gd:185-187` exercises, with a `# See also: autoload/c_bridge.gd:542` line saying the reconstructed view never carries the key; a frame carrying both keys counts a row once because the rows loop updates `_owners` first and the delta loop then reads `was == now`. Stated unchanged: `add_beat`, `lines()`, `banner()`, `advance`, `clock_text`, `BANNER_S` 4.0, `MAX_LINES` 6, the texts "Structure captured" and "Structure lost" (the S-235 play's `structure_lines` detector reads them), the `hud.events` inventory key (`tactical_view.gd:638`, a count), `TickerBeats` and its `beat` signal; no new qa key, no pipe or header change, no new number. Tests first (`tests/test_vg_e_a8feed.gd`, writer a different agent than the builder, `extends SceneTree` after the A8 test's shape; the live path is `CBridge._reconstruct_tactical` as `tests/test_c_bridge_vg.gd:107-124` already calls it, so no pipe and no desktop driver): [LIVE] a full frame (two structures, id 5 owner 1 and id 6 owner 0, `strongpoints: []`, local faction 0) through `_reconstruct_tactical` then `feed` gives no line (control); a delta `{"type": "tactical", "full": false, "tick": 21, "structures_changed": [{"id": 5, "owner": 0}]}` through `_reconstruct_tactical` gives a view with no `structures_changed` key and both rows (control that the test rides the real view) and after `feed` exactly one line of kind `captured` (the red assertion on the built tree, pasted: `A8FEED: R-127-04 one owner flip through the bridge is one 'captured' line; got []`); a delta flipping id 6 to owner 1 gives one more line of kind `lost`; a delta changing only `cap` on id 5 adds no line (no re-beat); [SKIP] the same flip on an id listed in the full frame's `strongpoints` adds no Structure line, with its control inside the same test (the same flip on a non-strongpoint id gives one `captured`, as [LIVE]); the [SKIP] assertion is green before and after the fix and is kept to pin the skip (`test_vg_e_e1m.gd` proves it only on raw deltas); [GUARD] a raw delta without a `structures` key after a full frame that holds an own id gives no line and leaves `_owners` unchanged; [WIRED] through `tactical_view.tscn` (`on_frame` twice with the full view then the flipped view) `qa_state()["inventory"]["hud"]["events"]` reads 1 (wired, not just tested); positive control: [LIVE] and [WIRED] are red on main plus the E1M branch for the stated reason and green on `tests/test_vg_a_events_feed.gd`'s raw-delta path, which is not edited (the row: "no edit to the A8 test"); no existing test encodes the superseded behaviour, so nothing retires. Element inventory of the shot: `hud.events` >= 1, `hud.objectives` >= 1, `hud.clock` non-empty, and the Events label text in the scene tree holds exactly one line starting "Structure captured" (read as the S-235 play reads it, `client.tree()`), no line outside the structure, objective and strongpoint beats (R-104-01 "nothing else at launch"). The S-127 play is a new file written by the test-writer tier (`TEST_WRITER` Sonnet 5.5, a different agent than the builder), red first on the built tree with the failing line pasted, and carries its own copy of `structure_lines` with a control (a line list holding a "Structure captured" line is detected, one without is not), as the S-235 play does (`S-235.py:91`). Shot (replaces the row's S-235 re-run, which needs `S-235.py:157` narrowed and cannot promise a flip in frame): the S-127 play `tests/qa/desktop/plays/E/S-127.py` (R-127-01, R-127-04): from the first tactical frame with the ticker empty, select the army by drag, attack-move to the nearest enemy structure that is not a strongpoint, `step_ticks` in blocks of 25 up to `DRAFT-a8feed-play-budget`, stop at the first Events line starting "Structure captured"; none by the cap is RED and a finding, never a skip or a labelled shot; bring the captured structure on screen (`project`, else `_edge_scroll_to`) and `moment(c, "S-127", "structure_captured")` is the shot, queued for Westley as the S-127 verdict shot; the lane's next look piece waits on his word. Pre-flight by the test writer, before the play is written: the play's mission must contain an enemy structure that is not a strongpoint (E1M's skip suppresses every strongpoint id, and `csrc/strongpoint.c:47-51` builds strongpoint rows by structure index, so a map whose every structure is a strongpoint cannot beat in play); the writer verifies this by reading the mission's `structures` rows against its `strongpoints` rows from a dump, names the mission and seed, and records the target's id, position and measured march distance in the play's header and a ledger row; if none exists the writer stops and reports (the shot then needs another source, an Architect ruling). `qa_state()` carries no per-structure rows (`tactical_view.gd:648` is a count only) and none is added: the play takes the target's position from that dump. `DRAFT-a8feed-play-budget` is set by the test writer from the dump: the measured march distance in ticks plus the S-235 margin (`DRAFT-s235-budget` 5920 over its measured march) and R-127-01's 10 s capture; the writer records it in the ledger as a named draft (Westley vetoes), and no number is fixed here. STOP rules: the builder stops on any need to edit `c_bridge.gd`, `tactical_view.gd`, `ticker_beats.gd` or any test; a flip that reaches the ticker twice is a finding, not a dedupe patch.

**F-S143, the common era set (owner `VG-F-S143`, depends_on `VG-F-S241` and `HARNESS-userdir`).** [B R-143-01: the match may traverse only eras every peer owns, in chronological order] [B R-143-03] The local player's owned eras are the installed packs, `scenes/era_library/era_pack_source.gd` `manifests()` (era_order order, `test_only` skipped, ids the manifest `era_id` strings), filtered by `UIDataBus.is_era_accessible(era_id)` (`autoload/ui_data_bus.gd:33`; the mock source `autoload/sim_bridge_data_source.gd:70-74` answers false for an id it does not list, so on main the list is `[settled_reach]`, an entitlement stub). If that list is empty or lacks `EraConfig.ACTIVE_ERA_ID` the builder stops with `spec-gap: owned era list <value>` and chooses no other source. The exchange: each member sends one lobby chat message `{"t": "eras", "ids": [era ids]}` when it joins (a guest on `lobby_joined`; the host records its own); the host keeps a table by lobby member id and publishes the intersection as lobby data key `playable_eras` (a `var_to_str` list) after every membership or table change; a leaving member drops its row; a malformed `eras` message is ignored; `on_chat` (`:261-272`) keeps handing every other message to `net_match`; a guest shows its own set until the keys arrive. The host also publishes the whole table as lobby data key `era_table` (a `var_to_str` Dictionary {member id (String): [era ids]}) beside `playable_eras` after every change, so a member that joins after the others' one-shot messages reads every row from the key: the chat messages are the input, the two keys are the output, and a guest's "peers" list is complete once the keys arrive (rev 13a, Architect 2026-10-19). A member whose message has not arrived is left out of the intersection, not counted as an empty set. Malformed means: `ids` not an Array, any item not a String, or an empty Array (every member owns the active era). An id the local game has no pack for stays in the member's row and in the intersection; the line sorts known ids by era_order first, then unknown ids alphabetically. A message from a sender that is not a lobby member is ignored. The host publishes its keys from `_on_lobby_created` (`:201`), never from `host_create` (`:188`): Steam's createLobby is asynchronous. Test seam `--net-local-eras <id,id>` (read with `OS.get_cmdline_user_args()` in `lobby.gd`, the way `autoload/net/local_lobby_backend.gd:38-41` reads `--net-local-id`) overrides the local owned list. The era line, node `EraLine` (`:136-137`), reads exactly "Eras: you <list> · peers <list> · playable <list>" with the middle dot U+00B7: each list the ids comma-space separated in era_order; "peers" is the union of the other members' eras and reads "none" with no other member; "playable" is the intersection over every member, the local one included. The fixed sentence "Limited to the era set every player has." is replaced; a pin on it is a superseded ruling that the ruler moves under a `TD-VG-F` row. `qa_state` gains `eras` = {member id (String): [era ids]} for every member the lobby knows, the local one included, and `playable_eras` = the intersection, and keeps `owned_eras` (= `eras[local]`) and `common_eras` (= `playable_eras`) under those names because `S-143.py:28-30` reads them and no play assertion is loosened. Tests first: `tests/test_vg_f_lobby_eras.gd` (one process, FakeBackend: the line's exact text with one member; a second member's `eras` message narrows `playable_eras` and the line; that member leaving widens it back; a malformed message changes nothing; positive control: the w4 lobby cases stay green) and a second two-process case in `S-143.py` on `P.pair_games` (host and guest as real processes, Q-F-1): the guest launched with `--net-local-eras` naming one era, the host owning two through the same seam, both lobbies showing the narrowed playable set. That case needs a guest extra-args hook in `tests/qa/desktop/plays/F/_play.py` `pair_games` (`:89-113`), which is why `VG-F-S143` owns that file and waits on `HARNESS-userdir`.
