**VG-C2-5-glowkey (galaxy view reports its glow radius; base piece for VG-C2-5-scope; owners `scenes/galaxy/galaxy_view.gd` (inside `qa_state()` only) and `tests/test_galaxy_view.gd`; depends_on `VG-C2-5-patch` and `VG-B25` (the queued owner of the same `qa_state()` lines; VG-B25 first, as the row says; VG-C2-5-glowkey then lands before VG-C2-5-scope); Architect, implementation, Westley may veto; Tool: Workflow (one piece on one client file, ends in a headless Godot test, not a frame: question 2).** [B R-106-02@4d4bb5] [P scenes/galaxy/galaxy_view.gd:318-363] [P scenes/galaxy/galaxy_adapter.gd:33-48] [P scenes/galaxy/galaxy_view.gd:256-260] [P scenes/galaxy/legacy_world_view_data.gd:43-48] [P scenes/galaxy/territory_field.gd:26] [P scenes/galaxy/territory_field.gd:196-217] [P scenes/galaxy/shaders/field_bake.gdshader:63-64] [P scenes/galaxy/orbit_camera.gd:65] [P scenes/galaxy/orbit_camera.gd:83-97] Finding (audit4 G1): `qa_state()` reports `systems_px`, `region_colours`, `pick_radius_px` and `shell_renderer.significance` and no pixel radius for a region's glow, so a detector that must know whether a dot sits under a neighbour's shell has nothing to read, and the scope piece owns only the test file. The key is the own-faction kernel radius, not the drawn edge: the shell's visible edge is the iso surface at `ISO` 0.6 (`shell_field.gdshader:22`), which lies inside the kernel radius for a lone system, and the iso-surface fraction is `DRAFT-c25-glow-edge`'s job; and the field bakes each system once per faction `f`, normalised by that faction's largest radius (`field_bake.gdshader:62-64`), so a rival faction's field scales the same system by the rival's `M_f` and the seam between two shells is driven by that rival term, which this key does not report. The field this reports: the adapter feeds each owned region a territory of radius `sig` (`galaxy_adapter.gd:48`; `sig * scale` during a conquest tween, `galaxy_view.gd:260`, scale 1.0 at rest), stored as `territories[str(i)]["radius"]` (`legacy_world_view_data.gd:43-48`); the field bakes it as a kernel of world radius `r_i = INFLUENCE_RADIUS * (0.6 + 0.4 * clamp(radius_i / M_f, 0, 1))`, `INFLUENCE_RADIUS` 14 wu (`territory_field.gd:26,90`) and `M_f` the largest stored radius among the systems of the same owner (`territory_field.gd:217`, `field_bake.gdshader:63-64`). The one change, in `qa_state()` only: a new key `glow_radius_px`, a Dictionary from region id (int, the same keys as `owners`) to float: for an owned region (`owners[i] >= 0`) that has a territory in `_adapter.territories`, `r_i` as above projected at the dot: `glow_radius_px[i] = (project(p_i + right * r_i) - project(p_i)).length()` with `p_i` the region's position, `project` the rig's `project` (`orbit_camera.gd:87`, the same call `systems_px` uses) and `right` the camera's world-space right axis (`_camera_rig.get_camera().global_transform.basis.x`, `orbit_camera.gd:65`); 0.0 for a neutral region or one with no territory (no shell, nothing to exclude by; DRAFT-c25-glow-neutral-zero, Architect); a region the camera cannot see (`is_behind`, `orbit_camera.gd:83`) is absent from `glow_radius_px` as it is from `systems_px`. The key reads positions, owners, the adapter's territories and the camera only, never the baked field, so it is the same under the dummy renderer as on the GPU. Stated unchanged: every existing key, the plants list, `PICK_RADIUS_PX`, the field, the shaders, the adapter and `set_territory`. Tests first (by a different agent than the builder, in `tests/test_galaxy_view.gd`, a new check `_check_glow_radius_px_S106_R106_02` called after `_check_systems_px_S106_R106_01` (`:88`)): (1) `glow_radius_px` is present and its key set equals the key set of `systems_px`; (2) for every region with `owners[i] >= 0` the value is positive, and for `owners[i] < 0` it is 0.0 (the fixture `snapshot_seed1_tick10.json` has 80 regions, 51 owned by 15 owners, 29 neutral); (3) the value is consistent with the formula from the state the test can read: with `w = pixels per world unit` measured as `(project(p_i + right) - project(p_i)).length()` through `qa_camera`'s rig, `glow_radius_px[i]` equals `14 * (0.6 + 0.4 * sig_i / max_sig_owner) * w` within `DRAFT-c25-glowkey-tol` 0.5 px (sig from `shell_renderer.significance`, max per owner from the same dict); (4) `qa_state()` survives `JSON.stringify` with the key (the existing `_check_qa_state_json_safe` covers it once the key exists); positive control: on main (the built tree before this piece) check (1) fails with "qa_state has no glow_radius_px" (the failing assertion line is pasted in the tests report), so the test is red for the stated reason; a mutant that reports the unprojected world radius fails (3); a mutant that normalises by the global largest radius instead of the per-owner largest (the fixture has 15 owners, so it differs) fails (3); a mutant that reports a positive value for a neutral region fails (2). Retired tests: none; the `BASE_KEYS` tests (`tests/test_vg_b_b14.gd:24`, `tests/test_vg_b_b4.gd:23`) check that listed keys are present, not that no extras exist, and none lists the new key, so none breaks. A piece with no frame: no shot, no look stage; the next frame is VG-C2-5-scope's gate run. No number beyond the named drafts `DRAFT-c25-glowkey-tol` 0.5 px and `DRAFT-c25-glow-neutral-zero` 0.0, and the numbers `DRAFT-c25-glow-edge` (1.0, ledger) and the report-only fractions of `DRAFT-c25-glow-edge-sizing` (1.0, 0.8, 0.4, ledger) that the scope paragraph uses.

## 2. Interfaces fixed (K0's `architecture.md` table; every test is written against this)

**Unit object** (per tick, full and delta): `{id,faction,type,tier,x,y,hp,layer,facing,target,state,acd?,tl?,ld?,sight?}`. `tier` int, 0 until a source exists. `state` int, always present (never omitted): value 1 fired during this call's steps (any of the n), 2 braced (guard/hold, `om_unit_is_braced`), 4 in layer transit (`tl` set); OR-ed; 0 otherwise. `acd` int: the tick the ability is ready (omitted when ready and never used). `tl` int layer target, `ld` int done tick: present only during a transit. `sight` int wu: own faction only. Deltas carry a unit when any of these changed.

**`structures`** (full frame; deltas `structures_changed` with the changed fields and `structures_removed` ids): `{id,type,owner,x,y,hp,stage,cap,nst,next_type,period}`; `cap` 0..1000 capture progress; `nst` the next spawn tick; `period` ticks between spawns.

**`events`** (per tick, never diffed; accumulated over `n>1` with their tick; cap 512 per call; `events_dropped` int when the cap is hit): `[kind,a,b,tick]`, kinds `0 fire(a shooter, b target)`, `1 ability(a unit, b ability id)`, `2 kill(a unit, b killer or -1)`, `3 capture(a structure, b new owner)`. With `fog_filter` on, an event is sent when `a` or `b` is visible to the local faction.

**`profiles`** (once per full frame): `[{type,max_hp,range,interval,sensor,ability,acd_max,aoe}]`.

**`objectives`** (per tick): `[{i,progress,done}]`, `done` 0 open, 1 complete, 2 failed.

**`map`** (full frames only, as today): `cover[{x,y,r,h,class}]` class 0 small (<150 wu) 1 medium (<400) 2 huge; `obstacles[{x,y,r}]`.

**Dumps** (once per mission): `terrain_dump` gains `heights` (hex LE int32 cm, `cells_w*cells_h`); `space_field_dump` (K-d), `lots_dump` (K-e) shapes written in their amendments.

**Verbs**: `command` gains `set_layer {units, layer}`; `activate_ability` as today; `mission_start` gains `fog_filter` (K-h).

**`qa_state` inventory keys**: `fog_layer`, `objective_markers`, `cover_cells`, `obstacles`, `structures`, `shots_in_flight`, `wrecks`, `squads`, `abilities_fx`, `ambient`, `loading_overlay`, `hud.{clock,objectives,selection,events,strip,comb,reinforcements}`, `units_px`, `objects_px`, `ground3d`, `shell_renderer`, `settlement_lots`, `strongpoints`, `map_markers`.

**Node names**: `UnitVisuals`, `UnitSquads`, `MapLayer`, `ObjectiveMarkers`, `FogLayer`, `LayerView`, `StructuresLayer`, `Hud/{Objectives,Selection,Comb,Events,Reinforcements,Strip,Clock}`, `Fx/{Shots,Deaths,Abilities,Ambient}`, `LoadingOverlay`, `Ground3D`, `ShellRenderer`, `SettlementLayer`, `StrongpointLayer`, `MapMarkers`, `TickerBeats`, `SpaceLayer`.

Story plays `tests/qa/desktop/plays/<lane>/<story>.py`; the review queue and ledger rows as rev 1.

## 3. The rulebook

`2026-10-07-visible-game-rulebook.md` rev 2: row 22 off the do-not-touch list for `state` (RP by K-a quoting this table), `tier` stays; rows 33–37 added for K-h (`test_tactical_delta_reconstruction.gd` first-frame two-faction control and enemy averaging; `test_pipe_assign_commander.py:85-87`; `test_pipe_tactical_tier_state_reserved.py:64-65`; `test_pipe_macro_group_orders.py:59-60`; `test_pipe_tactical_delta_frame.py:21`): with `fog_filter` off (the default in tests) they stand; K-h's own tests set the flag.

## 4. Self-healing

As rev 2, plus: every Westley-threshold check is written in its own `*_park_*` test file; when it is red the clerk leaves that file off the candidate, keeps it on `push/vg-parked`, and lists it in the landing report (the footprint fence only warns on owned files missing from a diff); the code it covers lands. A stopped piece parks itself and its dependents; a parked VG-VIEW link hands `tactical_view.gd` to the next link. A contract double-reject parks its dependents. A lane is dropped only when its clerk has no landable candidate after two check failures of non-parked pieces. `look` is regression-mode and warn-only until its threshold lands.

Rev 9 (method rev 2): the in-script ruling agent that rev 1 promised and never spawned now exists as the ruler role (`workflows_2026-10-07/rules.md` section (e)); a builder's `test-defect:` stop goes to it before anything reaches the Architect, and only a case outside the rulebook's decision procedure (new rule meaning, a threshold, a contradiction between spec and bible) becomes a one-page brief for the `architect` agent. Test writers verify their premises on the built tree before builders start (section (d)); the pre-gate runs before every push and before the gate lock (section (a) and (b)); a lane fans out only after its skeleton's shot is approved.

## 5. Guard pieces (`VG-GUARD`, separate test writer)

As rev 2; G1's `look` compares against main's frames at the same play moment (planted-blank control); warn-only (metric reported, frames attached) until Westley's threshold lands.

## 6. Owner rows

As rev 2 with: VG-CONTRACT owns `unit.c`, `tactical_support.c`, `mission.c`, `fog.c`, `tactical_move.c`, `tactical_mapgen.c`, `terrain.c` whole while K-a is in flight (then they return to B10/B9/PP-fog/B12/PP-mapgen/TERRAIN-1 or the lane rows); `VG-CONTRACT-E1` (K-e; E1's clerk); C10's six-file handoff A → C; E1i after K-a; plays per lane; the VG-VIEW chain A12 → F5 → C5 → E1p → D hook with the hand-on rule.

## 7. Liberties (tuning only)

Q-193-02 thresholds (0.10/0.22/0.38/0.58 onto five stages; Westley approves with the product); Q-237-01 closed as superseded; rubble rebuild 10 s; cover class 150/400 wu (applied in the sim); flyer altitude layer floor + 150 wu with a shadow disc; ground colour band ramp + steep tint + walkable; objective threshold beats at 25/50/75/100%; event cap 512 per call. Routed, not liberties: R-188-05, S-246, Q-219-02, Q-173-01/02, Q-167-03, Q-232-01 (the layer keys and narrowing), the tier source, D8's band, FIELD-EQ/REFILL/pixel/`look` thresholds, the 280 cell cap, Q-180-01. E2's tuning numbers are listed when E2 is spec'd.

## 8. Budget and kill rule

Rev 8 planned 118 pieces at 117M tokens with a 150M ceiling; the run used 847M raw (141M at cache weighting) for 135 pieces, $1,487 at list, $1,130 of it the two Fable sessions (`retro_2026-10-10/tokens.md`). Rev 9: no token or dollar cap (Westley 2026-10-10). What is kept: per agent, stop past 120k context; a coordinator chat restarts past about 150k; the record clerk writes each lane run's cost (tokens and dollars from the workflow result, Fable consults counted) into the programme file's scorecard, so the next spec is sized from a measured lane run and not from a guess. Kill rule: two lanes dropped (section 4's definition), or Westley's word. Gates 12 by day, 25 by night; lab at 4 workers by day, runs at night.

## 9. What Westley does

The go line this spec needs: "The visible game programme builds Spec'd stories; the Definition of Ready and the open-flag rule are waived for the visual layer (E-16, E-18, E-12, E-22's open flags listed); blocked stories stay listed and are not claimed Seen." Then: the routed questions via the parallel chat; the story queue; the Mac build and codesign; the Spacewar match; the Steam dashboard.

Rev 9: what waits on Westley is the "Now" table in ROADMAP section 3, at most six rows, ordered by what each unblocks; the Dispatcher adds a row only when one frees. While he is away only the programme file's "No eyes needed" list runs. His verdict on a queued shot is one word per row (approved or rejected, with any note) in the Dispatcher chat; the record clerk writes it into `story_reviews/<lane>.md` and flips `stories.md` on approved.

**PROTO-scale (prototype gate; Tool: Workflow for the starting point, then Westley iterates with a Sonnet chat that edits the mockup directly, his standing word for this prototype; owner new `scenes/mockups/scale/` (`scale_mockup.tscn`, `scale_mockup.gd`, `camera_rig.gd` copied from `scenes/mockups/units/camera_rig.gd` then set to the tactical rig's numbers, `kits/sizes.json`, `README.md`), new `scripts/mockups/scale_sizes.py`, new `tests/test_mockup_scale.gd`; depends_on none; no shot queued: Westley runs it).** [W 2026-10-21: "This prototype should show all units/things/landscapes shown in the game, and allow me to zoom. This includes all different types of units, a box representing their size (doesn't need to be actual objects), objective sizes, building sizes, mountain sizes, layout piece sizes, strategic position sizes, everything all lined up and interactable."] [W 2026-10-21: "Scaling seems off, units appear tiny compared to environment, it looks like whole armies could fit through the cracks."] [P scenes/mockups/_shared/README.md:3] [P scenes/ground3d/camera_rig_3d.gd:9-33] [P csrc/om_constants.h:479-630] A 3D scene on a flat grid of 100 wu cells (a line per cell, a heavy labelled line per 1000 wu), every sized thing in the game drawn as a labelled box, disc or ring at its code size, in rows the camera can fly along: units (the six types; each a 30 wu box, two collision radii of 15, with the drawn body disc 11.5 and shadow 10 inside, the flyer copy 150 above; rings for sensor 300 to 1000 and weapon range 200 to 1200, toggled); structures (a lot 100 × 100 × 60; manifest buildings at 100 wu per cell: workshop 3 × 3, dwelling, hut and outpost 2 × 2; the strongpoint placeholder 2 cells wide by 3 high (spec); the reinforcement capture disc 300; the production lattice 30 wu rings to 150); mission (objective cover disc 400, layer mask 600, hazard 500; the ground pads home 1200, forward 800, objective 600, resource 400 (spec); a choke 180 and 600 wide; town spacing 4000, resource clearance 2400, base inset 2400 as measured bars); terrain (one cell; the five band steps for a 4000 peak; a 35° slope wedge; the 8000, 12000 and 24000 canvases as dimmed ground rectangles); space (the five sheets 1000 apart; plate cell 1600, rubble cell 400, passage minimum 300, vision tube 1500, mass radius 2200, pocket clearing 600). Each label: name, size in wu, the constant's file, and "(spec)" when the number exists only in a spec. The camera is the tactical rig's: pitch 50, fov 75, zoom 150 to 40000, default 3000; keys 1 to 9 toggle rows, L labels, R rings, F cycles fov 45/60/75, P pitch 40/50/60/70; a HUD line shows height, pitch, fov and px per wu; clicking an element prints its line to the HUD. `kits/sizes.json` is generated by `scripts/mockups/scale_sizes.py`, which reads the constants from `csrc/om_constants.h`, `csrc/om_internal.h`, the era manifests and the `.gd` constants it names, so the mockup never drifts from code; spec-only numbers are a literal table in the script marked spec. `qa_state()` lists every element with its wu size and its screen size in px at the current camera; `PLANTS` and the `QADriver` child per the shared contract. Run line in the README: `~/.local/bin/godot.real --path . --display-driver x11 --rendering-driver opengl3 res://scenes/mockups/scale/scale_mockup.tscn`.
